📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: International journal of cancer  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-07  |  DOI: 10.1002/ijc.70736  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

p16 过表达和 人乳头瘤病毒 在 1000 多种阴茎癌中呈阳性。

p16 Overexpression and Human Papillomavirus Positivity in More Than 1000 Penile Cancers.
作者： Marianne Gardar Stærk, Birgitte Grønkær Toft, Christina Stilling, Johanna Elversang, Sanne Kjær-Frifeldt, Sonja Höhle Hjulskov, Sanja Stifter-Vretenar, Marie Aarslev Schou, Mikael Aagaard, Peter Meidahl Petersen
摘要中文翻译摘要 阴茎癌是一种严重的疾病,很大一部分与高危(hr)人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)有关。P16 在临床诊断中用作 HPV 的免疫组织化学标记物。关于阴茎癌中 p16 的 患病率 的基于人群的数据很少。我们在大量丹麦阴茎鳞状细胞癌 (SCC) 人群中研究了 p16 阳性的 患病率 以及 p16 与 hrHPV DNA 的组合。丹麦病理学数据库对 1995 年至 2020 年期间诊断的病例进行了鉴定,并对肿瘤样本进行了 HPV DNA 检测(INNO-LiPA 检测)。免疫组织化学 p16 染色后,病理学家对载玻片进行评估。计算 p16 患病率 并根据日历时间和组织学评估组合的 p16 和 hrHPV DNA 状态(双阳性、双阴性、仅 p16 阳性和仅 hrHPV DNA 阳性)。通过计算估计的年度百分比变化来评估时间趋势。在 1028 个阴茎 SCC 中,42.8% 为 p16 阳性,37.8% 为双阳性。我们发现 患病率 在研究期间没有显着变化。在具有 HPV 相关组织学特征的鳞状细胞癌中观察到最高的 患病率 双阳性,尤其是基底样细胞癌 (63.6%–87.5%)。在 97.9% 的双阳性 SCC 中检测到 9 价 HPV-疫苗 (HPV16/18/31/33/45/52/58) 涵盖的 hrHPV 基因型。基于这项基于大规模人群的研究,我们证明了 p16 和 hrHPV DNA 之间存在良好的相关性,并且 患病率 随着时间的推移(1995-2020 年)没有发生实质性变化。几乎所有双阳性 SCC 都含有 hrHPV 基因型,疫苗接种 可能可以预防该基因型,这表明丹麦的阴茎癌未来可能会减少。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨p16阳性以及p16与高危型(hr)HPV DNA联合检测在丹麦大量阴茎鳞状细胞癌(SCC)人群中的情况,以解决阴茎癌中关于p16的流行病学数据不足的问题。
设计与对象
本研究纳入了1995年至2020年间诊断的1028例阴茎鳞状细胞癌(SCC),并对肿瘤样本进行了HPV DNA检测和p16免疫组化染色。
核心方法
病例从丹麦病理数据库中识别,肿瘤样本通过INNO-LiPA检测方法进行HPV DNA检测。计算了p16的阳性率,并根据时间顺序和组织学类型评估了p16与hrHPV DNA的联合状态。通过计算估计的年百分比变化来评估时间趋势。
主要结果
在 1028 个阴茎 SCC 中,42.8% 为 p16 阳性,37.8% 为双阳性。在研究期间没有观察到 患病率 的显着变化。在具有 HPV 相关组织学特征的 SCC 中观察到最高的 患病率 双阳性,尤其是基底细胞样细胞 (63.6%–87.5%)。几乎所有双阳性 SCC 都含有 hrHPV 基因型,该基因型被 9 价 HPV 疫苗 覆盖。
解释与创新
本研究显示p16与hrHPV DNA之间存在良好的相关性,并且在时间(1995–2020)范围内p16阳性率没有显著变化。几乎所有双重阳性SCC均携带可被疫苗预防的hrHPV基因型,这表明丹麦未来阴茎癌的发病率可能下降。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,p16阳性与hrHPV DNA在阴茎鳞状细胞癌中存在强关联性,而9价HPV疫苗覆盖的hrHPV基因型的高阳性率提示通过疫苗接种有可能降低阴茎癌的发病率。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其观察性质和缺乏随机化。样本量较大,但研究时间相对较短,这可能影响研究结果的普遍适用性。由于研究设计为观察性,且存在潜在的混杂因素,证据强度中等。

p16 Overexpression and Human Papillomavirus Positivity in More Than 1000 Penile Cancers.

Authors: Marianne Gardar Stærk, Birgitte Grønkær Toft, Christina Stilling, Johanna Elversang, Sanne Kjær-Frifeldt, Sonja Höhle Hjulskov, Sanja Stifter-Vretenar, Marie Aarslev Schou, Mikael Aagaard, Peter Meidahl Petersen
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Penile cancer is a severe disease, and a large proportion is related to high‐risk (hr) human papillomavirus (HPV). P16 is used as an immunohistochemical marker of HPV in clinical diagnostics. Population‐based data on the prevalence of p16 in penile cancer is scarce. We investigated the prevalence of p16 positivity, and p16 in combination with hrHPV DNA, in a large population of Danish penile squamous cell carcinomas (SCC). Cases diagnosed during 1995–2020 were identified in the Danish Pathology Databank, and tumor samples were tested for HPV DNA (INNO‐LiPA assay). After immunohistochemical p16‐staining, the slides were evaluated by expert pathologists. p16 prevalence was calculated and the combined p16 and hrHPV DNA status (double positivity, double negativity, p16 positive only, and hrHPV DNA positive only) was assessed according to calendar time and histology. Time trends were assessed by calculating estimated annual percentage changes. Of 1028 penile SCCs, 42.8% were p16 positive and 37.8% were double positive. We found no significant changes in prevalences over the study period. The highest prevalence of double positivity was observed in SCCs with HPV‐associated histological features, especially basaloid (63.6%–87.5%). hrHPV genotypes covered by the 9‐valent HPV‐vaccine (HPV16/18/31/33/45/52/58) were detected in 97.9% of double positive SCCs. Based on this large population‐based study, we demonstrate a good correlation between p16 and hrHPV DNA and no substantial changes in the prevalences over time (1995–2020). Nearly all double positive SCCs harbored a hrHPV genotype that could potentially be prevented by vaccination, pointing to a likely future decrease in penile cancer in Denmark.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the prevalence of p16 positivity and p16 in combination with high-risk (hr) HPV DNA in a large population of Danish penile squamous cell carcinomas (SCC) to address the scarcity of population-based data on p16 in penile cancer.
Design and population
The study included 1028 penile SCCs diagnosed between 1995 and 2020, with HPV DNA testing and p16 immunohistochemical staining performed on tumor samples.
Core methods
Cases were identified in the Danish Pathology Databank, and tumor samples were tested for HPV DNA using the INNO-LiPA assay. p16 prevalence was calculated, and the combined p16 and hrHPV DNA status was assessed according to calendar time and histology. Time trends were assessed by calculating estimated annual percentage changes.
Main results
Of 1028 penile SCCs, 42.8% were p16 positive and 37.8% were double positive. No significant changes in prevalences were observed over the study period. The highest prevalence of double positivity was observed in SCCs with HPV-associated histological features, especially basaloid (63.6%–87.5%). Nearly all double positive SCCs harbored a hrHPV genotype covered by the 9-valent HPV vaccine.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrates a good correlation between p16 and hrHPV DNA and no substantial changes in the prevalences over time (1995–2020). Nearly all double positive SCCs harbored a hrHPV genotype that could potentially be prevented by vaccination, pointing to a likely future decrease in penile cancer in Denmark.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that p16 positivity and hrHPV DNA are strongly associated in penile SCCs, and the high prevalence of hrHPV genotypes covered by the 9-valent HPV vaccine indicates a potential for reducing penile cancer incidence through vaccination.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational nature and the lack of randomization. The sample size is large, but the study period is relatively short, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational design and the potential for confounding factors.

长非编码 RNA 作为 诊断 的生物标志物和 HPV 相关 宫颈癌 的预后:系统范围界定综述。

Long non-coding RNAs as biomarkers for diagnosis and prognosis in HPV-associated cervical cancer: A systematic scoping review.
作者： Farzaneh Bilan, Behboud Jafari, Jalal Gharesouran, Amir Hossein Kiani Darabi, Sahar Tajeddini, Maryam Rezazadeh, Soudeh Ghafouri-Fard
摘要中文翻译背景:长非编码 RNA (lncRNA) 是 HPV 驱动的癌变的新兴调节因子,在 宫颈癌 (CC) 中具有潜在的诊断和预后价值。临床和机械证据的当代综合对于优先考虑转化开发的候选者是必要的。方法:我们对评估 HPV 相关 CC 中 lncRNA 的研究进行了系统范围审查。各大数据库检索共 556 条记录;经过重复数据删除(n = 220)和筛选后,对 154 篇全文进行了评估,其中 27 篇研究符合纳入标准。没有进行正式的质量或偏倚风险评估,这与范围界定审查框架一致。提取的数据包括研究标识符、lncRNA 身份、HPV 关联、样本类型/大小、HPV 阳性疾病中的表达、诊断指标(AUC/敏感性/特异性)、预后指标(生存统计)和机制发现。结果:27 项研究描述了 29 种独特的 lncRNA,其中 75% 的研究上调,25% 的研究下调。组织来源的分析占主导地位(21 项研究),其中 13 项研究采用细胞系实验,少数研究采用循环分析(血清 3 项;血浆 2 项)。13/27 的研究(∼48%)报告了诊断性能,22/27 的研究(∼81.5%)报告了预后关联。MALAT1 和 GATA6-AS1 在两项独立研究中分别进行了评估,而 HOTAIR 在两项具有不同类型证据的研究中进行了调查。机制数据一致表明 HPV E6/E7 依赖性调节、ceRNA/miRNA 海绵作用、细胞周期控制和上皮间质转化。结论:汇总的证据表明,HPV 响应性 lncRNA 代表了 诊断 和预后的有希望的候选生物标志物,并且可能值得作为治疗靶点进行进一步研究;然而,在临床转化之前需要在独立的 队列研究 中进行前瞻性验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在综合长链非编码 RNA (lncRNA) 作为 诊断 潜在生物标志物和 HPV 相关 宫颈癌 预后的临床和机制证据,满足当代审查的需要,以优先考虑转化开发的候选者。
设计与对象
进行了系统范围审查,筛选了 556 条记录,删除了 220 条重复记录,并评估了 154 篇全文,最终得出 27 项研究符合纳入标准。
核心方法
评估 HPV 相关 宫颈癌 中 lncRNA 的研究是通过搜索主要数据库确定的,提取了有关 lncRNA 身份、HPV 关联、样本类型/大小、HPV 阳性疾病中的表达、诊断指标、预后措施和机制发现的数据。
主要结果
27 项研究描述了 29 种独特的 lncRNA,其中 75% 上调,25% 下调。13 项研究 (48%) 报告了诊断性能,22 项研究 (81.5%) 报告了预后相关性。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,HPV 响应性 lncRNA 代表了 诊断 和预后的有希望的候选生物标志物,值得作为治疗靶点进行进一步研究,但需要在独立的 队列研究 中进行前瞻性验证。
科研与公卫意义
汇总的证据表明,HPV 响应性 lncRNA 可能作为 诊断 和 HPV 相关 宫颈癌 预后的有前景的生物标志物,可能有助于 监测、诊断 和 治疗 策略。
局限与证据强度
该审查没有进行正式的质量或偏倚风险评估,并且由于缺乏独立的 队列研究 前瞻性验证,研究结果受到限制。

Long non-coding RNAs as biomarkers for diagnosis and prognosis in HPV-associated cervical cancer: A systematic scoping review.

Authors: Farzaneh Bilan, Behboud Jafari, Jalal Gharesouran, Amir Hossein Kiani Darabi, Sahar Tajeddini, Maryam Rezazadeh, Soudeh Ghafouri-Fard
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Long non-coding RNAs (lncRNAs) are emerging regulators of HPV-driven carcinogenesis with potential diagnostic and prognostic value in cervical cancer (CC). A contemporary synthesis of clinical and mechanistic evidence is necessary to prioritize candidates for translational development. METHODS: We conducted a systematic scoping review of studies assessing lncRNAs in HPV-associated CC. Searches across major databases yielded 556 records; after de-duplication (n = 220) and screening, 154 full texts were assessed, and 27 studies met the inclusion criteria. No formal quality or risk-of-bias assessment was performed, consistent with the scoping review framework. The extracted data included study identifiers, lncRNA identity, HPV association, sample type/size, expression in HPV-positive disease, diagnostic metrics (AUC/sensitivity/specificity), prognostic measures (survival statistics), and mechanistic findings. RESULTS: The 27 studies described 29 unique lncRNAs, which were upregulated in 75% and downregulated in 25% of the studies. Tissue-derived analyses predominated (21 studies), with cell line experiments in 13 studies and circulating assays in a minority (serum 3; plasma 2). Diagnostic performance was reported in 13/27 studies (∼48%), and prognostic associations were reported in 22/27 (∼81.5%). MALAT1 and GATA6-AS1 were each evaluated in two independent studies, whereas HOTAIR was investigated in two studies with different types of evidence. Mechanistic data consistently implicated HPV E6/E7-dependent regulation, ceRNA/miRNA sponging, cell cycle control, and epithelial-mesenchymal transition. CONCLUSION: The compiled evidence indicates that HPV-responsive lncRNAs represent promising candidate biomarkers for diagnosis and prognosis, and may warrant further investigation as therapeutic targets; however, prospective validation in independent cohorts is required before clinical translation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to synthesize clinical and mechanistic evidence on long non-coding RNAs (lncRNAs) as potential biomarkers for diagnosis and prognosis in HPV-associated cervical cancer, addressing the need for a contemporary review to prioritize candidates for translational development.
Design and population
A systematic scoping review was conducted, screening 556 records, de-duplicating to 220, and assessing 154 full texts, resulting in 27 studies meeting the inclusion criteria.
Core methods
Studies assessing lncRNAs in HPV-associated cervical cancer were identified through searches across major databases, with data extracted on lncRNA identity, HPV association, sample type/size, expression in HPV-positive disease, diagnostic metrics, prognostic measures, and mechanistic findings.
Main results
Twenty-seven studies described 29 unique lncRNAs, with 75% upregulated and 25% downregulated. Diagnostic performance was reported in 13 studies (48%), and prognostic associations were reported in 22 studies (81.5%).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that HPV-responsive lncRNAs represent promising candidate biomarkers for diagnosis and prognosis, warranting further investigation as therapeutic targets, but prospective validation in independent cohorts is required.
Scientific and public-health significance
The compiled evidence indicates that HPV-responsive lncRNAs may serve as promising biomarkers for diagnosis and prognosis in HPV-associated cervical cancer, potentially aiding in surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The review did not perform formal quality or risk-of-bias assessments, and the findings are limited by the lack of prospective validation in independent cohorts.

Journal: Vaccine  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-06  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.vaccine.2026.129114  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 92, 129114
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

HPV 疫苗接种 计划对同性恋、双性恋和其他男男性行为者肛门 HPV 感染的影响:英格兰专业性健康服务的多中心增强 监测 研究。

Impact of the HPV vaccination programme on anal HPV infection in gay, bisexual and other men who have sex with men: multi-site enhanced surveillance study in specialist sexual health services in England.
作者： Marta Checchi, Kavita Panwar, Clare E. French, Hamish Mohammed, Simon Beddows, Matthew Hickman, Kate Soldan
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
我们通过监测一段时间内特定类型 HPV 感染的 患病率 来评估该计划的影响。
设计与对象
残留直肠拭子样本是从 2017-19 年(第 1 阶段)和 2021-24 年(第 2 阶段)期间在 10 个性健康服务(SHS)接受衣原体和/或淋病筛查的年龄≤45 岁的 GBMSM 中收集的。
核心方法
使用内部多重 PCR 和基于 Luminex 的基因分型测试对匿名样本进行类型特异性 HPV DNA 测试。
主要结果
在第 1 阶段和第 2 阶段之间,肛门 HPV16/18 感染的 患病率 下降了 36%。
解释与创新
GBMSM的HPV 疫苗接种开始八年后,GBMSM的疫苗型HPV的患病率已经下降。
科研与公卫意义
现有数据无法准确估计 疫苗 的有效性,这可能是由于 疫苗接种 漏报、群体保护和 疫苗接种 吸收偏差的综合影响。
局限与证据强度
这些发现虽然是生态学的,但对于该目标计划来说是个好兆头,可以实现减少 GBMSM 中 HPV 相关疾病和减少健康不平等的目标。

Impact of the HPV vaccination programme on anal HPV infection in gay, bisexual and other men who have sex with men: multi-site enhanced surveillance study in specialist sexual health services in England.

Authors: Marta Checchi, Kavita Panwar, Clare E. French, Hamish Mohammed, Simon Beddows, Matthew Hickman, Kate Soldan
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: A national human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination programme for gay, bisexual, and other men who have sex with men (GBMSM) in England began in 2016. We evaluated the impact of this programme by monitoring the prevalence of type-specific HPV infections over time. METHODS: Residual rectal swab specimens were collected from GBMSM aged ≤45 years undergoing chlamydia and/or gonorrhoea screening at 10 sexual health services (SHSs) between 2017-19 (Phase 1) and 2021-24 (Phase 2). Specimens were linked to data on sociodemographic characteristics, previous STI diagnoses, and HPV vaccination reported to the UK Health Security Agency's (UKHSA's) national STI surveillance system. Anonymised specimens were tested for type-specific HPV DNA using an in-house multiplex PCR and Luminex-based genotyping test. We compared the prevalence of vaccine-type HPV in each phase, and in those with and without any reported HPV vaccinations. RESULTS: Data from 5,787 rectal swab specimens with HPV testing results were analysed: 3,291 in Phase 1 and 2,496 in Phase 2. There was a 36% decline in the prevalence of anal HPV16/18 infection between Phase 1 and Phase 2. Overall, the prevalence of HPV16/18 was similar in GBMSM with (16.6%; 95% CI 15.2-18.1) and without reported HPV vaccination (15.8%; 95% CI 14.5-17.1). CONCLUSION: Eight years after HPV vaccination of GBMSM began, the prevalence of vaccine-type HPV in GBMSM has declined. Available data did not permit accurate estimation of vaccine effectiveness, likely due to the combined effects of underreporting of vaccinations, herd protection, and bias in vaccination uptake. These findings, albeit ecological, bode well for this targeted programme meeting its aim of reducing HPV-related diseases among GBMSM and reducing health inequalities.

循环肿瘤 HPV DNA 再现作为基于放疗的 治疗 治疗 HPV 相关口咽癌后复发的早期指标:对 232 名患者的 2000 多项评估进行分析。

Circulating tumor HPV DNA reappearance as an early indicator of recurrence after radiotherapy-based treatment for HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer: analysis of more than 2000 assessments in 232 patients.
作者： Iwona Agnieszka Jabłońska, Tomasz Wojciech Rutkowski, Urszula Kacorzyk, Marek Kentnowski, Natalia Vydra, Agnieszka Maria Mazurek
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
循环肿瘤 人乳头瘤病毒 DNA (ctHPV DNA) 是 HPV 阳性口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 和原发不明头颈癌 (CUP) 中一种有前景的血液 监测 生物标志物。
设计与对象
2012 年至 2024 年间在我们机构接受治疗且基线时血浆中可检测到 ctHPV DNA 的 OPSCC/CUP 患者纳入研究。
核心方法
所有患者均使用定量 PCR 进行前瞻性连续 ctHPV DNA 监测 检测。
主要结果
在 220 名获得完全放射学和分子学缓解的患者中,ctHPV DNA 再现对于检测临床证实的复发显示出 90.3% 的敏感性、97.9% 的特异性 (Sp)、87.5% 的阳性预测值 (PPV) 和 98.4% 的阴性预测值 (NPV)。
解释与创新
在基于每次测试间隔的分析中,灵敏度在 45 天时达到峰值 90.9%,而在所有时间间隔(14-180 天)内,特异性和 NPV 仍然很高(Sp 97.6%-98.8%,NPV ≥ 98.9%)。
科研与公卫意义
基于纵向 qPCR 的 ctHPV DNA 监测可以通过识别短期复发风险极低的患者,并在部分患者进行临床确认之前提供分子复发的早期信号,可以补充基线 ctHPV DNA 阳性 HPV 相关 OPSCC/CUP 中的标准后 治疗 监测。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取基于检索到的全文证据。消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Circulating tumor HPV DNA reappearance as an early indicator of recurrence after radiotherapy-based treatment for HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer: analysis of more than 2000 assessments in 232 patients.

Authors: Iwona Agnieszka Jabłońska, Tomasz Wojciech Rutkowski, Urszula Kacorzyk, Marek Kentnowski, Natalia Vydra, Agnieszka Maria Mazurek
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Circulating tumor human papillomavirus DNA (ctHPV DNA) is a promising blood-based surveillance biomarker in HPV-positive oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) and head-and-neck carcinoma of unknown primary (CUP). This study aimed to evaluate the diagnostic performance of ctHPV DNA-based molecular testing for detecting disease recurrence. METHODS: Patients with OPSCC/CUP treated at our institution between 2012 and 2024 who had detectable ctHPV DNA in plasma at baseline were included. All patients underwent prospective serial ctHPV DNA surveillance using quantitative PCR. RESULTS: 2928 assessments were performed in 232 patients, including 2192 surveillance tests. Among 220 patients who achieved complete radiologic and molecular response, ctHPV DNA reappearance showed 90.3% sensitivity, 97.9% specificity (Sp), 87.5% positive predictive value (PPV), and 98.4% negative predictive value (NPV) for detecting clinically confirmed recurrence. CtHPV DNA reappearance preceded clinical recurrence in 58.1% of recurrent cases. In per-test interval-based analyses, sensitivity peaked at 90.9% at 45 days, while specificity and NPV remained high (Sp 97.6%-98.8%, NPV ≥ 98.9%) across all time intervals (14-180 days). CtHPV DNA positivity at 1 month after radiotherapy-based treatment completion was strongly associated with worse overall survival (HR 19.1, 95% CI 7.5-48.8) and disease-free survival (HR 15.4, 95% CI 6.7-35.4), whereas the result obtained immediately after treatment had limited prognostic value. CONCLUSIONS: Longitudinal qPCR-based ctHPV DNA monitoring may complement standard post-treatment surveillance in baseline ctHPV DNA-positive HPV-associated OPSCC/CUP by identifying patients at very low short-term risk of recurrence and by providing an early signal of molecular recurrence before clinical confirmation in a subset of patients.

Prevalence of high-risk human papillomavirus genotypes and associated factors among HIV-infected women in a reproductive age group attending antiretroviral therapy in Nekemte town, Oromia, Ethiopia.

Prevalence of high-risk human papillomavirus genotypes and associated factors among HIV-infected women in a reproductive age group attending antiretroviral therapy in Nekemte town, Oromia, Ethiopia.

Authors: Rundase Gebeyehu, Seifu Gizaw, Desta Amensisa, Tadesse Bekele, Belay Merkeb, Fedasan Alemu, Gadisa Cewaka
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Human papillomavirus is a deoxyribonucleic acid virus transmitted primarily sexually. Infection with high-risk human papillomavirus is recognized as the key cause of cervical cancer that is leftover as a public health problem globally. Human immunodeficiency virus infected women of a reproductive age who are on antiretroviral therapy are at higher risk due to debilitated immunity. Therefore, this study aimed to determine the prevalence of high-risk human papillomavirus and associated factors. METHODS: An institutional-based cross-sectional study that included 317 HIV-infected women of a reproductive age group attending antiretroviral therapy was conducted in Nekemte town employing a systematic random sampling technique. A structured questionnaire and record reviews were used to gather the data. Cervical specimens were collected from June to August 2024 and tested at Nekemte Public Health Research and Referral Laboratory using the "Abbott real-time high-risk HPV DNA assay". The collected data were entered into Epi-data version 3.1 and exported to the Statistical Package for Social Sciences version 26 for analysis. Descriptive statistics were used to summarize the data, and logistic regression analyses were performed to identify the determinants. The odds ratio with a 95% confidence interval was computed to assess the presence and degree of association. Following multivariable model fitting, variables with a p-value < 0.05 after adjustment for multiple testing were considered statistically significant. RESULTS: The prevalence of high-risk human papillomavirus was 32.2% [95% CI: 27.1-37.6%], while a single genotype infection with HR-HPV-16, HR-HPV-18 and other HR-HPV accounted for 12.75%, 8.82% and 62.7% respectively. On a multivariate logistic regression, having sex before 18 years (adjusted odds ratio [AOR] =3.54, 95%CI: 1.87-6.69), multiple lifetime sex partners (AOR = 3.69, 95%CI: 1.96-6.96), and a history of sexually transmitted infection (AOR = 3.81, 95%CI: 1.93-7.51) were associated with higher odds of infection. Conversely, a longer duration on antiretroviral therapy was associated with lower odds of infection (AOR = 0.18, 95%CI: 0.06-0.54). CONCLUSION AND RECOMMENDATION: The prevalence was high in the study area. Having multiple sex partners, early sexual debut, history of sexually transmitted infections, and shorter duration on antiretroviral therapy were determinants demanding inclusive intervention efforts. Health authorities hence, need to reinforce routine human papillomavirus screening and health education for human immunodeficiency virus infected women of a reproductive age who are on antiretroviral therapy.

Core methods

Main results

Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

质量改进合作伙伴关系旨在提高 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 费率:从 9 岁开始活动。

Quality Improvement Partnership to Improve Human Papillomavirus Vaccine Rates: Start at Age 9 Campaign.
作者： Lauren E Ng, Jessica P Fenton, Linda Mermelstein, Lori Ellis, Randi Trope
摘要中文翻译简介:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染和 HPV 相关癌症可以用 疫苗接种 预防。在 9-10 岁开始 HPV 疫苗 系列与 疫苗接种 吸收和系列完成的改善相关。这项质量改进 (QI) 计划旨在通过实施循证干预措施,提高 9 至 10 岁儿童的首剂 HPV 疫苗接种 感染率。方法:从 2021 年至 2024 年期间每年至少进行 1 次健康儿童就诊的 9-10 岁既定患者的电子健康记录 (EHR) 中收集数据。基线数据从 2021 年 1 月到 2022 年 5 月获得。干预措施包括提供者教育、采用全系统“从 9 点开始”政策、面向患者的教育材料以及 EHR 修改以整合 HPV 疫苗 订单9岁及以上的患者。使用 P 图表评估每月 疫苗接种 趋势。结果:共纳入 9315 名 9-10 岁患者。在采用“9 点开始”政策并结合有针对性的教育干预措施和 EHR 整合后,接受首次 HPV 疫苗 的符合资格患者的百分比从 2% 的基线增加到 14%。P 图表显示特殊原因变异与 2 项 QI 干预措施一致,表明持续的流程转变。用作平衡措施的破伤风、白喉和无细胞百日咳 (Tdap) 疫苗接种 率在整个研究期间保持稳定。结论:协调的系统级 QI 方法与早期 HPV 疫苗 启动的显着进展相关,支持在 9 岁时启动 疫苗接种 作为促进及时 疫苗接种 和加强预防性健康结果的有效策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过实施质量改进计划来解决 9 至 10 岁儿童中 HPV 疫苗接种 率低的问题。人乳头瘤病毒 感染和 HPV 相关癌症可以用 疫苗接种 预防。
设计与对象
开展了一项质量改进计划,通过提供者教育、全系统“从 9 点开始”政策、面向患者的教育材料和 EHR 修改,提高 9 至 10 岁儿童的首剂 HPV 疫苗接种 率。数据收集自 2021 年至 2024 年间每年至少进行过一次健康儿童就诊记录的 9 至 10 岁患者的电子健康记录。
核心方法
基线数据从 2021 年 1 月到 2022 年 5 月获得。干预措施包括提供者教育、采用全系统“从 9 点开始”政策、面向患者的教育材料以及修改 EHR 以整合 9 岁及以上患者的 HPV 疫苗 订单。使用 P 图表评估每月 疫苗接种 趋势。
主要结果
共纳入 9315 名 9-10 岁患者。在采用“9 点开始”政策并结合有针对性的教育干预措施和 EHR 整合后,接受首次 HPV 疫苗 的符合资格患者的百分比从 2% 的基线增加到 14%。P 图表显示特殊原因变异与 2 项 QI 干预措施一致,表明持续的流程转变。用作平衡措施的破伤风、白喉和无细胞百日咳 (Tdap) 疫苗接种 率在整个研究期间保持稳定。
解释与创新
协调的系统级 QI 方法与早期 HPV 疫苗 启动的显着进展相关,支持在 9 岁时启动 疫苗接种 作为促进及时 疫苗接种 和加强预防性健康结果的有效策略。HPV 疫苗接种 率显着增加,并且随着时间的推移持续增加。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,在 9-10 岁时开始 HPV 疫苗 系列与 疫苗接种 吸收和系列完成的改善相关。这种方法可以在其他环境中实施,以提高 HPV 疫苗接种 率并减轻 HPV 相关疾病的负担。
局限与证据强度
研究设计的局限性包括缺乏对照组和可能存在混杂因素。由于该研究的观察性质以及数据收集对电子健康记录的依赖,证据强度中等。

Quality Improvement Partnership to Improve Human Papillomavirus Vaccine Rates: Start at Age 9 Campaign.

Authors: Lauren E Ng, Jessica P Fenton, Linda Mermelstein, Lori Ellis, Randi Trope
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Human papillomavirus (HPV) infections and HPV-related cancers are preventable with vaccination. Initiating the HPV vaccine series at ages 9-10 years is associated with improved vaccination uptake and series completion. This quality improvement (QI) initiative aimed to increase first-dose HPV vaccination rates among 9- to 10-year-olds through the implementation of evidence-based interventions. METHODS: Data were collected from electronic health records (EHRs) of established patients aged 9-10 years with at least 1 documented well-child visit annually from 2021 to 2024. Baseline data were obtained from January 2021 through May 2022. Interventions included provider education, adoption of a system-wide "Start at 9" policy, patient-facing educational materials, and EHR modifications to integrate HPV vaccine orders for patients aged 9 years and older. Monthly vaccination trends were evaluated using a P-chart. RESULTS: A total of 9315 patients aged 9-10 years were included. The percentage of eligible patients receiving their first HPV vaccine increased from a baseline of 2% to 14% following the adoption of the "Start at 9" policy in conjunction with targeted educational interventions and EHR integration. A P-chart demonstrating special cause variation aligned with 2 QI interventions, indicating a sustained process shift. Tetanus, diphtheria, and acellular pertussis (Tdap) vaccination rates, used as balancing measures, remained stable throughout the study period. CONCLUSIONS: A coordinated, systems-level QI approach was associated with significant gains in early HPV vaccine initiation, supporting initiation of vaccination at age 9 years as an effective strategy to promote timely vaccination and strengthen preventive health outcomes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the low vaccination rates of HPV among 9- to 10-year-olds by implementing a quality improvement initiative. Human papillomavirus infections and HPV-related cancers are preventable with vaccination.
Design and population
A quality improvement initiative was conducted to increase first-dose HPV vaccination rates among 9- to 10-year-olds through provider education, a system-wide 'Start at 9' policy, patient-facing educational materials, and EHR modifications. Data were collected from electronic health records of patients aged 9-10 years with at least one documented well-child visit annually from 2021 to 2024.
Core methods
Baseline data were obtained from January 2021 through May 2022. Interventions included provider education, adoption of a system-wide 'Start at 9' policy, patient-facing educational materials, and EHR modifications to integrate HPV vaccine orders for patients aged 9 years and older. Monthly vaccination trends were evaluated using a P-chart.
Main results
A total of 9315 patients aged 9-10 years were included. The percentage of eligible patients receiving their first HPV vaccine increased from a baseline of 2% to 14% following the adoption of the 'Start at 9' policy in conjunction with targeted educational interventions and EHR integration. A P-chart demonstrating special cause variation aligned with 2 QI interventions, indicating a sustained process shift. Tetanus, diphtheria, and acellular pertussis (Tdap) vaccination rates, used as balancing measures, remained stable throughout the study period.
Interpretation and novelty
The coordinated, systems-level QI approach was associated with significant gains in early HPV vaccine initiation, supporting initiation of vaccination at age 9 years as an effective strategy to promote timely vaccination and strengthen preventive health outcomes. The increase in HPV vaccination rates was notable and sustained over time.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that initiating the HPV vaccine series at ages 9-10 years is associated with improved vaccination uptake and series completion. This approach can be implemented in other settings to improve HPV vaccination rates and reduce the burden of HPV-related diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study design limitations include the lack of a control group and the potential for confounding factors. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and the reliance on electronic health records for data collection.

人乳头瘤病毒 阴茎鳞状细胞癌的现状:一个已确定的生物标志物,但尚未解答问题。

Human Papillomavirus Status in Penile Squamous Cell Carcinoma: An Established Biomarker With Unanswered Questions.
作者： Xianjie Xiu, Kaat Vandermaesen, Annekatrien Semal, Maarten Albersen
摘要中文翻译阴茎鳞状细胞癌 (PSCC) 是一种罕见但具有侵袭性的恶性肿瘤,近几十年来,其 发病率 在多个高收入地区呈上升趋势。很大一部分归因于高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV)。HPV 阳性和 HPV 阴性 PSCC 在肿瘤生物学和肿瘤微环境中存在显着差异,大多数研究报告 HPV 阳性疾病的预后更好,但 HPV 状态的预后和治疗预测价值仍存在争议且尚未解决。Stærk 等人。提供了一个大型、高质量和同质的数据集来解决这些问题:26 年来在丹麦诊断出 1000 多个阴茎 SCC,这些数据来自丹麦全国病理学数据库,具有集中的 HPV 基因分型和盲法专家 p16 评分。

Human Papillomavirus Status in Penile Squamous Cell Carcinoma: An Established Biomarker With Unanswered Questions.

Authors: Xianjie Xiu, Kaat Vandermaesen, Annekatrien Semal, Maarten Albersen
Original abstract (English)
Penile squamous cell carcinoma (PSCC) is a rare but aggressive malignancy whose incidence has been rising across several highincome regions in recent decades.A substantial fraction is attributable to high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV).HPV-positive and HPV-negative PSCC differ markedly in tumour biology and in the tumour microenvironment, and most studies report better outcomes for HPV-positive disease-yet the prognostic and therapeutic predictive value of HPV status remains contested and unresolved.Stærk et al. provide a large, high-quality and homogeneous dataset to address these questions: more than one thousand penile SCCs diagnosed in Denmark over 26 years, drawn from the nationwide Danish Pathology Databank with centralised HPV genotyping and blinded expert p16 scoring.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Penile squamous cell carcinoma (PSCC) is a rare but aggressive malignancy whose incidence is rising, and a substantial fraction is attributable to high‐risk human papillomavirus (HPV). The prognostic and therapeutic predictive value of HPV status in PSCC remains contested and unresolved, prompting the study to address these questions.
Design and population
The authors assembled a large, high‐quality and homogeneous dataset comprising more than one thousand penile SCCs diagnosed in Denmark over a 26‐year period. Cases were drawn from the nationwide Danish Pathology Databank and subjected to centralized HPV genotyping and blinded expert p16 scoring.
Core methods
Specimens from the Danish Pathology Databank were centrally genotyped for HPV and evaluated by blinded expert assessment of p16 expression to determine HPV‐related status.
Main results
The supplied evidence does not report any quantitative or qualitative results regarding HPV status or clinical outcomes in the studied cohort.
Interpretation and novelty
The supplied evidence does not report the authors’ interpretation of findings or any claimed novelty.
Scientific and public-health significance
The supplied evidence does not report implications for surveillance, diagnosis, treatment, vaccination, risk assessment, or other public‐health actions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report study limitations or an assessment of the strength of the evidence.

口咽癌中低危型人乳头瘤病毒的临床病理特征及分子特征

Clinicopathologic features and molecular characterization of oropharyngeal carcinomas harboring low-risk human papillomavirus.
作者： Michel Kmeid, Christopher C Griffith, Akeesha A Shah, Justin A Bishop
摘要中文翻译尽管高危型(HR)人乳头瘤病毒(人乳头瘤病毒,HPV)与口咽癌(OC)的关联已得到充分证实,但低危型(LR)HPV在这些肿瘤中的作用及其生物学意义仍不明确。在本研究中,我们报道了4例通过原位杂交(ISH)检测出LR-HPV阳性的OC病例。这些病例发生于3名男性和1名女性,平均年龄为67.8岁。所有肿瘤均表现出乳头状/外生性结构,其中2例显示明显的间质浸润。形态学上,肿瘤表现出从具有相对低级别细胞学特征的角化性肿瘤到以非角化性肿瘤为主且具有高级别细胞学特征的广泛谱系。1例肿瘤中观察到腺体分化。所有肿瘤均出现空泡化细胞改变,其中2例观察到显著的上皮内中性粒细胞。2例肿瘤中存在可能的鳞状乳头瘤前病变。所有肿瘤均对LR-HPV RNA ISH呈阳性,而对HR-HPV RNA ISH呈阴性。3例肿瘤的p16免疫组化呈阴性,1例呈不确定。对两例肿瘤进行测序后确认了HPV6 DNA的存在,测序还发现了涉及TERT、PIK3CA、FAT1、KRAS和EP300的改变。罕见的OC可能表现出转录活性的LR-HPV表达,并携带在传统HPV独立鳞状细胞癌中常见的分子改变。这些肿瘤在形态学上与鳞状乳头瘤、类似Schneiderian的乳头瘤、腺鳞癌、黏液表皮样癌以及DEK::AFF2重排癌存在重叠。尽管LR-HPV在这些肿瘤中的意义尚不明确,但这些发现扩展了在口咽部遇到的HPV相关病变的谱系,并为未来机制研究奠定了基础。

问题与背景

设计与对象
在两个接受测序的肿瘤中均证实了 HPV6 DNA,此外还发现了涉及 TERT、PIK3CA、FAT1、KRAS 和 EP300 的改变。
核心方法
3 例 p16 免疫组化呈阴性,1 例可疑。
主要结果
所有肿瘤均表现出乳头状/外生结构,其中 2 个肿瘤表现出明显的间质浸润。
解释与创新
罕见的OC可能表现出转录活性的LR-HPV表达,并携带在传统HPV独立鳞状细胞癌中常见的分子改变。
科研与公卫意义
这些肿瘤在形态学上与鳞状乳头瘤、类似Schneiderian的乳头瘤、腺鳞癌、黏液表皮样癌以及DEK::AFF2重排癌存在重叠。
局限与证据强度
尽管LR-HPV在这些肿瘤中的意义尚不明确,但这些发现扩展了在口咽部遇到的HPV相关病变的谱系,并为未来机制研究奠定了基础。

Clinicopathologic features and molecular characterization of oropharyngeal carcinomas harboring low-risk human papillomavirus.

Authors: Michel Kmeid, Christopher C Griffith, Akeesha A Shah, Justin A Bishop
Original abstract (English)
Although the association between high-risk (HR) subtypes of human papillomavirus (HPV) and oropharyngeal carcinoma (OC) is well established, the role and biologic significance of low-risk (LR) HPV in these tumors remains poorly understood. In this series, we present 4 OCs with LR-HPV positivity by in situ hybridization (ISH). The cases occurred in 3 males and 1 female with a mean age of 67.8 years. All tumors demonstrated a papillary/exophytic architecture, and 2 showed overt stromal invasion. Morphologically, the tumors exhibited a spectrum ranging from keratinizing neoplasms with relatively low-grade cytologic features to predominantly non-keratinizing tumors with higher-grade cytology. Glandular differentiation was identified in one case. Koilocytic change was present in all tumors, while prominent intraepithelial neutrophils were identified in two cases. A possible squamous papilloma precursor lesion was present in two cases. All tumors were positive for LR-HPV RNA ISH and negative for HR-HPV RNA ISH. p16 immunohistochemistry was negative in 3 cases and equivocal in one. HPV6 DNA was confirmed in both tumors subjected to sequencing, which additionally identified alterations involving TERT, PIK3CA, FAT1, KRAS, and EP300. Rare OCs may demonstrate transcriptionally active LR-HPV expression and harbor molecular alterations commonly encountered in conventional HPV-independent squamous cell carcinoma. These tumors exhibit morphologic overlap with squamous papilloma, Schneiderian-like papilloma, adenosquamous carcinoma, mucoepidermoid carcinoma, and DEK::AFF2-rearranged carcinoma. While the significance of LR-HPV in these neoplasms remains uncertain, the findings expand the spectrum of HPV-associated lesions encountered in the oropharynx and provide a foundation for future mechanistic studies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Although the association between high-risk (HR) subtypes of human papillomavirus (HPV) and oropharyngeal carcinoma (OC) is well established, the role and biologic significance of low-risk (LR) HPV in these tumors remains poorly understood.
Design and population
HPV6 DNA was confirmed in both tumors subjected to sequencing, which additionally identified alterations involving TERT, PIK3CA, FAT1, KRAS, and EP300.
Core methods
p16 immunohistochemistry was negative in 3 cases and equivocal in one.
Main results
All tumors demonstrated a papillary/exophytic architecture, and 2 showed overt stromal invasion.
Interpretation and novelty
Rare OCs may demonstrate transcriptionally active LR-HPV expression and harbor molecular alterations commonly encountered in conventional HPV-independent squamous cell carcinoma.
Scientific and public-health significance
These tumors exhibit morphologic overlap with squamous papilloma, Schneiderian-like papilloma, adenosquamous carcinoma, mucoepidermoid carcinoma, and DEK::AFF2-rearranged carcinoma.
Limitations and evidence strength
While the significance of LR-HPV in these neoplasms remains uncertain, the findings expand the spectrum of HPV-associated lesions encountered in the oropharynx and provide a foundation for future mechanistic studies.

基于 HPV 的 宫颈癌 护理点筛查? Pluslife HPV 16/18/45 检测的临床前评估

HPV-Based Cervical Cancer Screening at the Point-of-Care? A Preclinical Evaluation of the Pluslife HPV 16/18/45 Assay
作者： Lara Noble, Lesley Scott, Danielle Gast, Lucia Hans, Pamela Michelow, Wendy Stevens
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基于人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)核酸的检测是宫颈癌筛查的首选方法。利用点对点(POC)技术可以提高在资源有限地区筛查的可及性。
设计与对象
在使用AccuTrakTM HPV Qualification Panel参考材料或残留液基细胞学样本制备的拭子,以及储存的、自取的阴道拭子样本上,对Pluslife HPV-16/18/45核酸测试卡在Pluslife平台上的实验室基础概念验证评估进行了研究。
核心方法
Pluslife HPV 16/18/45检测在制备和自取的阴道拭子样本中表现出与BD OnclarityTM HPV检测相似的性能。这些数据支持进一步对Pluslife HPV 16/18/45检测进行临床评估,作为快速、POC HPV筛查的解决方案。
主要结果
In 50 contrived clinical swabs, PPA was 100.0% (95% CI: 88.4, 100.0), NPA was 95.0% (95% CI: 75.1, 99.9), and overall agreement was 95.8% (95% CI: 87.7, 103.9). When stratified by cytology, the relative clinical sensitivity and specificity were 100.0% (100.0, 100.0) and 95.0% (95% CI: 64.4, 140.0) respectively. Amongst 24 self-collected vaginal swabs, the PPA and NPA were 92.3% (95% CI: 64.0, 99.8) and 100.0% (95% CI: 71.5, 100.0) respectively, with overall agreement of 91.7% (95% CI: 75.7, 107.6).
解释与创新
Pluslife HPV 16/18/45检测在制备和自取样本中均表现出高分析性能,表明其作为快速、点对点(POC)HPV筛查工具的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为进一步临床评估 Pluslife HPV 16/18/45 检测作为一种快速的 HPV 护理点筛查方法奠定了基础,这可以提高资源有限环境中 宫颈癌 筛查的可及性。
局限与证据强度
该研究为预临床研究,使用了非标签生物储存样本,这可能限制研究结果的普遍适用性。然而,分析性能指标稳健,PPA和NPA均较高,并且在120次测试中仅发现一个错误。

HPV-Based Cervical Cancer Screening at the Point-of-Care? A Preclinical Evaluation of the Pluslife HPV 16/18/45 Assay

Authors: Lara Noble, Lesley Scott, Danielle Gast, Lucia Hans, Pamela Michelow, Wendy Stevens
Original abstract (English)
Background: Human papillomavirus (HPV) nucleic acid-based testing is the preferred method for cervical cancer screening. Leveraging point-of-care (POC) technologies can increase accessibility to screening in resource-limited settings. However, POC technologies are designed for use with fresh specimens and obtaining pre-clinical data to motivate clinical trials can be difficult. A laboratory-based, proof-of-concept evaluation of the Pluslife HPV-16/18/45 Nucleic Acid Test Card on the Pluslife platform (Pluslife Biotech Co, Ltd., Guangzhou, Guangdong, China) was performed on swabs contrived using AccuTrakTM HPV Qualification Panel (SeraCare) reference material, or residual liquid-based cytology specimens, and stored, self-collected, vaginal swab specimens. Pluslife HPV 16/18/45 results were compared to paired BD OnclarityTM HPV (Becton Dickinson) results to provide analytical performance data (positive percentage agreement (PPA), negative percentage agreement (NPA), overall agreement (Cohen’s Kappa), and relative clinical sensitivity and specificity, all reported with 95 percent confidence intervals (95% CI)). The AccuTrakTM and LBC contrived swab reproducibility results were correct for both inter- and intra-day testing (100.0% agreement). In 50 contrived clinical swabs, PPA was 100.0% (95% CI: 88.4, 100.0), NPA was 95.0% (95% CI: 75.1, 99.9), and overall agreement was 95.8% (95% CI: 87.7, 103.9). When stratified by cytology, the relative clinical sensitivity and specificity were 100.0% (100.0, 100.0) and 95.0% (95% CI: 64.4, 140.0) respectively. Amongst 24 self-collected vaginal swabs, the PPA and NPA were 92.3% (95% CI: 64.0, 99.8) and 100.0% (95% CI: 71.5, 100.0) respectively, with overall agreement of 91.7% (95% CI: 75.7, 107.6). Only one error (cartridge fill) was noted in 120 tests performed. This pre-clinical study described methods for using off-label biorepository specimens. The Pluslife HPV 16/18/45 assay showed similar performance to the BD On-clarityTM HPV assay on both contrived and self-collected vaginal swab specimens. These data support further clinical evaluation of the Pluslife HPV 16/18/45 assay as a solution for rapid, POC HPV screening.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) nucleic acid-based testing is the preferred method for cervical cancer screening. Leveraging point-of-care (POC) technologies can increase accessibility to screening in resource-limited settings.
Design and population
A laboratory-based, proof-of-concept evaluation of the Pluslife HPV-16/18/45 Nucleic Acid Test Card on the Pluslife platform was performed on swabs contrived using AccuTrakTM HPV Qualification Panel reference material, or residual liquid-based cytology specimens, and stored, self-collected, vaginal swab specimens.
Core methods
The Pluslife HPV 16/18/45 assay showed similar performance to the BD OnclarityTM HPV assay on both contrived and self-collected vaginal swab specimens. These data support further clinical evaluation of the Pluslife HPV 16/18/45 assay as a solution for rapid, POC HPV screening.
Main results
In 50 contrived clinical swabs, PPA was 100.0% (95% CI: 88.4, 100.0), NPA was 95.0% (95% CI: 75.1, 99.9), and overall agreement was 95.8% (95% CI: 87.7, 103.9). When stratified by cytology, the relative clinical sensitivity and specificity were 100.0% (100.0, 100.0) and 95.0% (95% CI: 64.4, 140.0) respectively. Amongst 24 self-collected vaginal swabs, the PPA and NPA were 92.3% (95% CI: 64.0, 99.8) and 100.0% (95% CI: 71.5, 100.0) respectively, with overall agreement of 91.7% (95% CI: 75.7, 107.6).
Interpretation and novelty
The Pluslife HPV 16/18/45 assay demonstrated high analytical performance on both contrived and self-collected specimens, suggesting its potential as a rapid, point-of-care screening tool for HPV.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide a foundation for further clinical evaluation of the Pluslife HPV 16/18/45 assay as a rapid, point-of-care screening method for HPV, which could improve accessibility to cervical cancer screening in resource-limited settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was preclinical and used off-label biorepository specimens, which may limit the generalizability of the findings. However, the analytical performance metrics were robust, with high PPA and NPA, and only one error was noted in 120 tests performed.

委内瑞拉三个土著社区中人乳头瘤病毒基因型谱的差异:对疫苗选择和宫颈筛查的启示

Divergent Human Papillomavirus Genotype Spectra in Three Indigenous Communities of Venezuela: Implications for Vaccine Choice and Cervical Screening
作者： María Fátima Garcés, Jefferson Guzmán, Daniel Aranguren, Lina Figueira, Ricardo Blanch, Marwan Said Aguilar Mejía, Yacelli Bustamante Siberio, Juan Bautista De Sanctis, Alexis Hipólito García, Marwan Said Aguilar Mejia
摘要中文翻译关于委内瑞拉土著人群人乳头瘤病毒(人乳头瘤病毒)的基因型解析数据几乎缺失。我们对来自委内瑞拉La Guajira(Wayuu,n = 94)、Maniapure(Eñepa,n = 141)和Delta Amacuro(Warao,n = 25)的260名土著女性进行了多中心横断面研究。所有样本均按照单一协议处理,实时PCR可单独检测21种基因型;144名女性接受了常规细胞学检查。总体HPV患病率为55.4%(95% CI 49.3–61.3),高危型HPV患病率为51.2%(95% CI 45.1–57.2);阳性率在不同社区间存在差异(χ2 = 13.40,df = 2,p = 0.001),Delta Amacuro社区达到80.0%,Maniapure社区为59.6%,La Guajira社区为42.6%。检测到19种基因型。总体上,HPV-31最为常见(15.8%),其次是HPV-16(11.5%)和HPV-18(9.2%)。不同社区的基因型谱差异显著:Delta Amacuro社区以HPV-68(48.0%)和HPV-56(40.0%)为主,La Guajira社区为HPV-51(9.6%),Maniapure社区为HPV-31(20.6%)。51.4%的感染女性携带多种基因型。细胞学与qPCR之间的符合率极低(Cohen’s κ = 0.056),细胞学的敏感性为25.6%。HPV在这些社区中高度流行;流行的基因型谱远超HPV-16/18,且仅靠细胞学检查不足以作为有效的筛查策略。总之,九价疫苗疫苗更受推荐,因为它覆盖了土著社区感染女性的69.4%,而二价疫苗仅覆盖30.6%。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
关于委内瑞拉土著人群人乳头瘤病毒(人乳头瘤病毒)的基因型解析数据几乎缺失。
设计与对象
我们对来自委内瑞拉La Guajira(Wayuu,n = 94)、Maniapure(Eñepa,n = 141)和Delta Amacuro(Warao,n = 25)的260名土著女性进行了多中心横断面研究。
核心方法
所有样本均按照单一协议处理,实时PCR可单独检测21种基因型;144名女性接受了常规细胞学检查。
主要结果
总体HPV患病率为55.4%(95% CI 49.3–61.3),高危型HPV患病率为51.2%(95% CI 45.1–57.2);阳性率在不同社区间存在差异(χ2 = 13.40,df = 2,p = 0.001),Delta Amacuro社区达到80.0%,Maniapure社区为59.6%,La Guajira社区为42.6%。
解释与创新
检测到19种基因型。
科研与公卫意义
总体上,HPV-31最为常见(15.8%),其次是HPV-16(11.5%)和HPV-18(9.2%)。
局限与证据强度
不同社区的基因型谱差异显著:Delta Amacuro社区以HPV-68(48.0%)和HPV-56(40.0%)为主,La Guajira社区为HPV-51(9.6%),Maniapure社区为HPV-31(20.6%)。

Divergent Human Papillomavirus Genotype Spectra in Three Indigenous Communities of Venezuela: Implications for Vaccine Choice and Cervical Screening

Authors: María Fátima Garcés, Jefferson Guzmán, Daniel Aranguren, Lina Figueira, Ricardo Blanch, Marwan Said Aguilar Mejía, Yacelli Bustamante Siberio, Juan Bautista De Sanctis, Alexis Hipólito García, Marwan Said Aguilar Mejia
Original abstract (English)
Genotype-resolved data on human papillomavirus (HPV) are almost absent for Venezuelan indigenous populations. We conducted a multi-site cross-sectional study of 260 indigenous women from La Guajira (Wayuu, n = 94), Maniapure (Eñepa, n = 141), and Delta Amacuro (Warao, n = 25), Venezuela. All samples were processed under a single protocol, with real-time PCR resolving 21 genotypes individually; conventional cytology was available in 144 women. Overall HPV prevalence was 55.4% (95% CI 49.3–61.3) and high-risk HPV prevalence was 51.2% (95% CI 45.1–57.2); positivity differed across communities (χ2 = 13.40, df = 2, p = 0.001), reaching 80.0% in Delta Amacuro versus 59.6% in Maniapure and 42.6% in La Guajira. Nineteen genotypes were detected. HPV-31 was the most frequent overall (15.8%), then HPV-16 (11.5%) and HPV-18 (9.2%). The profiles differed markedly between communities: HPV-68 (48.0%) and HPV-56 (40.0%) in Delta Amacuro, HPV-51 (9.6%) in La Guajira, and HPV-31 (20.6%) in Maniapure. Multiple genotypes were found in 51.4% of infected women. Agreement between cytology and qPCR was negligible (Cohen’s κ = 0.056), with a cytology sensitivity of 25.6%. HPV is highly prevalent in these communities; the circulating genotype spectrum extends well beyond HPV-16/18, and cytology alone is not an adequate screening strategy. In conclusion, the nonavalent vaccine is preferred, as it covers 69.4% of infected women of the indigenous community, compared to 30.6% for the bivalent formulation.

HPV16 利用磷脂酶 C(s) 实现其 基因组 脱壳

HPV16 Utilizes Phospholipase C(s) for its Genome Egress
作者： Timothy R. Keiffer, Abida Siddiqa, Mireya Represa-Perez, Hiba Zabir, Anand Kushwaha, Martin Sapp, Katarzyna Zwolinska, Katarzyna Zwolińska
摘要中文翻译摘要 人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)的核内递送需要感染细胞经历有丝分裂。进入的HPV DNA由运输囊泡结构保护,在有丝分裂后变得不稳定。我们之前的研究表明,HPV的核内递送后平均4小时后病毒颗粒开始释放;因此,我们推测一种酶促过程参与降解这种运输囊泡,以允许病毒颗粒释放。几种磷脂酶C(PLC)同工酶,包括PLCδ3、PLCζ1和PLCL1,已在大规模siRNA筛选中与HPV感染相关,并且副腺病毒VP1衣壳蛋白具有磷脂酶酶活性。因此,我们假设细胞核内的磷脂酶介导HPV病毒颗粒从运输囊泡中释放。我们使用siRNA介导的敲低和CRISPR/Cas9敲除技术在HeLa和HaCaT细胞系中靶向特定的PLC同工酶。我们发现,靶向PLC同工酶PLCβ1、β4、δ3、δ4和γ1显著降低了HeLa和HaCaT细胞中的HPV感染,这通过基于荧光素酶的报告基因测定进行测量。我们还发现HPV16小蛋白L2与PLC同工酶β1、β4、δ1、δ3和δ4之间存在新的相互作用。此外,我们观察到在HeLa细胞感染后,敲除PLCβ4和δ4会延迟HPV病毒颗粒从核膜结合囊泡中的释放。我们提出,HPV利用磷脂酶C在核内递送后从其保护性囊泡结构中释放病毒颗粒。重要性 HPV进入的后期阶段涉及的细胞因子仍需阐明。先前的大规模siRNA筛选数据表明磷脂酶在HPV感染中发挥作用。我们在此基础上进一步发现,PLC同工酶β4和δ4对于HPV感染至关重要。我们还发现,PLCβ4-和PLCδ4-缺陷细胞中感染性的丧失与HPV病毒颗粒从运输囊泡释放缺陷相关,我们使用我们的差异染色技术证明了这一点。此外,我们还发现HPV16小蛋白L2与PLC同工酶β1、β4、δ1、δ3和δ4之间存在新的相互作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨 HPV 人乳头瘤病毒 在核内递送后如何利用磷脂酶 C (PLC) 同工型实现 基因组 脱壳的机制,填补了对 HPV 后期进入阶段涉及的细胞因子理解的空白。
设计与对象
研究使用 siRNA 干扰和 CRISPR/Cas9 敲除技术在 HeLa 和 HaCaT 细胞系中靶向特定的 PLC 同工型,评估其对 HPV 感染的影响。
核心方法
研究人员使用 siRNA 干扰和 CRISPR/Cas9 敲除技术靶向 PLC 同工型,并使用基于荧光素酶的报告基因测定法评估其对 HPV 感染的影响。他们还使用差异染色技术观察了 HPV 基因组 从运输囊泡的释放。
主要结果
靶向 PLC 同工型 PLCβ1、β4、δ3、δ4 和 γ1 显著降低了 HeLa 和 HaCaT 细胞中的 HPV 感染。PLCβ4 和 δ4 敲除延迟了 HPV 基因组 从核膜结合囊泡的脱壳。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为证据,表明 PLC 同工型在 HPV 基因组 脱壳中发挥关键作用,并发现了 HPV16 小蛋白 L2 与多个 PLC 同工型之间的新型相互作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为 HPV 基因组 释放的分子机制提供了见解,可能有助于开发针对病毒传播的靶向疗法或干预措施。
局限与证据强度
研究仅限于使用细胞系进行的体外实验,证据基于摘要中的数据,可能无法完全代表体内条件。对发现的信心程度为中等。

HPV16 Utilizes Phospholipase C(s) for its Genome Egress

Authors: Timothy R. Keiffer, Abida Siddiqa, Mireya Represa-Perez, Hiba Zabir, Anand Kushwaha, Martin Sapp, Katarzyna Zwolinska, Katarzyna Zwolińska
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Nuclear delivery of human papillomavirus (HPV) requires infected cells to undergo mitosis. Incoming HPV DNA is protected by a transport vesicle structure, which becomes unstable post-mitosis. We have previously shown that HPV genome egress takes on average 4 hours post-mitosis completion; thus, we propose that an enzymatic process is involved in degrading this transport vesicle to allow genome egress. Several phospholipase C (PLC) isoforms, including PLCδ3, PLCζ1, and PLCL1, have been previously implicated in HPV infection in large-scale siRNA screens, and the parvovirus VP1 capsid protein has phospholipase enzymatic activity. Therefore, we hypothesized that cellular phospholipases residing in the nucleus mediate egress of the HPV genome from transport vesicles. We utilized siRNA-mediated knockdown and CRISPR/Cas9 knockout techniques to target specific PLC isoforms in both HeLa and HaCaT cell lines. We found that targeting PLC isoforms PLCβ1, β4, δ3, δ4, and γ1 significantly decreased HPV infection in both HeLa and HaCaT cells, as measured using a luciferase-based reporter assay. We also discovered a novel interaction between HPV16 minor protein L2 and PLCs β1, β4, δ1, δ3, and δ4. Furthermore, we observed that knockout of PLCβ4 and δ4 delayed egress of the HPV genome from nuclear membrane-bound vesicles after infection of HeLa cells. We propose that HPV utilizes phospholipase Cs to achieve genome egress from its protective vesicular structure after nuclear delivery. Importance The cellular factors involved in the later stages of HPV entry remain to be elucidated. Previous data gleaned from large-scale siRNA screens implicated the involvement of phospholipases in HPV infection. We expanded upon these findings and discovered that the individual phospholipase C (PLC) isoforms β4 and δ4 are important for HPV infectivity. We further linked the loss of infectivity in PLCβ4- and PLCδ4-deficient cells to a defect in HPV genome release from its transport vesicle, which we demonstrated using our differential staining technique. Furthermore, we discovered a novel interaction between the minor protein L2 of HPV16 and PLC isoforms β1, β4, δ1, δ3, and δ4.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the mechanisms by which human papillomavirus (HPV) achieves genome egress from its transport vesicle after nuclear delivery, addressing a gap in understanding the cellular factors involved in the later stages of HPV entry.
Design and population
The study was conducted using siRNA-mediated knockdown and CRISPR/Cas9 knockout techniques in HeLa and HaCaT cell lines to target specific phospholipase C (PLC) isoforms and assess their impact on HPV infection.
Core methods
The researchers used siRNA-mediated knockdown and CRISPR/Cas9 knockout to target PLC isoforms and evaluated the effect on HPV infection using a luciferase-based reporter assay. They also employed a differential staining technique to observe genome release from transport vesicles.
Main results
Targeting PLC isoforms PLCβ1, β4, δ3, δ4, and γ1 significantly decreased HPV infection in both HeLa and HaCaT cells. Knockout of PLCβ4 and δ4 delayed the egress of the HPV genome from nuclear membrane-bound vesicles.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as evidence that phospholipase C (PLC) isoforms play a critical role in HPV genome egress, and they discovered a novel interaction between HPV16 minor protein L2 and several PLC isoforms.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide insights into the molecular mechanisms of HPV genome release, which could inform the development of targeted therapies or interventions aimed at preventing viral spread.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro experiments using cell lines, and the evidence is based on abstract-only data, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions. The confidence in the findings is moderate.

对于接受辅助生殖计划的夫妇,男性和女性同时感染人乳头瘤病毒可能与临床妊娠时间延长和活产率降低有关。

For couples in assisted reproduction programmes, concurrent male and female infection with human papillomavirus might be associated with a longer time to clinical pregnancy and a lower live birth rate.
作者： Jefferson Gardet, Armin Priam, Moncef Benkhalifa, Baptiste Demey, Rosalie Cabry, Dorian Bosquet, Fabien Saint
摘要中文翻译INTRODUCTION:我们在一项前瞻性先前研究中评估了不育男性人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)状态与精子异常(少精症、弱精症和/或畸形精子症)之间的关联。然而,关于HPV感染对妊娠率和活产率影响的研究报道较少。MATERIALS AND METHODS:2021年5月至10月期间,我们前瞻性地从461名因不育就诊的男性中收集了临床、实验室和感染相关数据(包括HPV状态)。随后,我们收集了这些男性伴侣的相同数据,以评估男性HPV感染和夫妇同时HPV感染对治疗结果的影响。妊娠结局采用Kaplan-Meier方法进行评估。RESULTS:共有78对夫妇接受了HPV感染筛查。与HPV+男性夫妇相比,HPV-男性夫妇的自发性及辅助性妊娠率更高(58.7% vs. 37.5%;p=NS),活产率也更高(50% vs.。自发性及辅助性妊娠率在HPV-夫妇中高于HPV+夫妇(60.5% vs. 37.1%;p=0.06),活产率也更高(51% vs.。然而,HPV+夫妇的临床妊娠时间并未显著长于HPV-夫妇(p=0.05)。CONCLUSION:接受不育评估的夫妇应系统性地进行HPV筛查。同时HPV阳性似乎与较低的妊娠可能性和较低的出生率有关。

For couples in assisted reproduction programmes, concurrent male and female infection with human papillomavirus might be associated with a longer time to clinical pregnancy and a lower live birth rate.

Authors: Jefferson Gardet, Armin Priam, Moncef Benkhalifa, Baptiste Demey, Rosalie Cabry, Dorian Bosquet, Fabien Saint
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: We assessed in a prospective previous study the association between the human papillomavirus (HPV) status of infertile men and the presence of sperm abnormalities (oligospermia, asthenospermia, and/or teratospermia). However, few studies have reported on the impact of HPV infection on pregnancy rates and live birth rates. MATERIALS AND METHODS: from May to October 2021, we prospectively collected clinical, laboratory, and infection-related data (including HPV status) from 461 men consulting for infertility. We subsequently collected the same data on the men's partners, to assess the impact of both male HPV infections and concurrent couple HPV infections on treatment outcomes. Pregnancy outcomes were assessed using the Kaplan-Meier method. RESULTS: 78 couples were screened for HPV infection. The rate of spontaneous and medically assisted pregnancies was higher among couples with an HPV- male than among couples with an HPV+ male (58.7% vs. 37.5%; p=NS), as was the live birth rate (50% vs. The spontaneous and medically assisted pregnancy rate was higher in HPV- couples than in HPV+ couples (60.5% vs. 37.1%; p=0.06), as was the live birth rate (51% vs. However, time to clinical pregnancy was not significatively longer in HPV+ couples than HPV- couples (p=0.05). CONCLUSION: Couples being assessed for infertility should be systematically screened for HPV. Concurrent HPV positivity appears to be associated with a lower likelihood of pregnancy and a lower birth rate.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

手术治疗生殖器和口咽部尖锐湿疣后长期随访及同时进行人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗接种对合并HPV-16或-18感染患者的影响

Long-Term Follow-Up After Surgical Treatment of Anogenital and Oropharyngeal Condyloma Acuminata with Simultaneous HPV Vaccination Initiation in Patients with Concurrent HPV-16 or -18 Co-Infection
作者： Joanna Wojciula, Marcin Bartoszewicz, Magdalena Wojciula, Piotr Sienkiewicz, Grzegorz Szewczyk, Piotr Fiedor
摘要中文翻译背景/目的:持续感染高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)基因型是生殖器和口咽部恶性肿瘤发生的重要原因。患有原发性或获得性免疫缺陷或接受免疫抑制治疗的患者,包括移植受者和罕见疾病患者,容易出现感染的特别严重临床表现。虽然HPV疫苗接种是主要的预防手段,但HPV相关疾病患者在手术治疗尖锐湿疣后仍面临再次感染或感染其他基因型的风险,可能从术前术后疫苗接种方案中获益。本研究旨在评估在合并高危基因型感染的尖锐湿疣患者中,手术治疗后同时进行HPV疫苗接种的长期结果。方法:350名尖锐湿疣患者,其HPV-16或-18合并感染经组织病理学检查和PCR基因分型确认(包括口腔、外阴、阴道、阴茎和肛周部位),其中一部分患者具有原发性或获得性免疫缺陷或慢性免疫抑制治疗方案,接受了手术治疗并同时接种了第一剂双价Cervarix疫苗(170名患者)或四价Gardasil疫苗(180名患者),后续在12个月内完成完整接种方案。在完成接种方案后6个月和12个月进行随访检查,包括HPV基因分型(16/18/31/35)。在36个月时进行标准临床检查,对可疑病例补充进行基因分型和活检/细胞学检查。观察持续进行至后续的5年、10年和超过15年。结果:在36个月随访中,有5%的患者需要移除尖锐湿疣,且在双价或四价疫苗组之间无显著统计学差异。未观察到细胞异型性或以鳞状细胞癌形式出现的恶性转化。基因分型未在随访检查中检测到HPV-16/18/31/35感染。结论:在这项未控制的单臂研究中,术前术后开始接种双价或四价HPV疫苗与尖锐湿疣临床复发率低及随访期间未检测到HPV-16/18/31/35感染相关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
持续感染高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)基因型是生殖器和口咽部恶性肿瘤发生的重要原因。
设计与对象
350名尖锐湿疣患者,其HPV-16或-18合并感染经组织病理学检查和PCR基因分型确认,接受了手术治疗并同时接种了第一剂双价Cervarix疫苗或四价Gardasil疫苗。
核心方法
患者在手术治疗期间接受了双价Cervarix疫苗(170名患者)或四价Gardasil疫苗(180名患者),并在后续12个月内完成完整接种方案。在完成接种方案后6个月和12个月进行随访检查,包括HPV基因分型(16/18/31/35)。在36个月时进行标准临床检查,对可疑病例补充进行基因分型和活检/细胞学检查。
主要结果
在36个月随访中,有5%的患者需要移除尖锐湿疣,且在双价或四价疫苗组之间无显著统计学差异。未观察到细胞异型性或以鳞状细胞癌形式出现的恶性转化。基因分型未在随访检查中检测到HPV-16/18/31/35感染。
解释与创新
在这项未控制的单臂研究中,术前术后开始接种双价或四价HPV疫苗与尖锐湿疣临床复发率低及随访期间未检测到HPV-16/18/31/35感染相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,术前术后开始接种HPV疫苗可能降低尖锐湿疣复发风险,并防止合并高危基因型感染的患者再次感染HPV-16、-18、-31或-35。
局限与证据强度
本研究为未控制的单臂研究,这限制了对术前术后疫苗接种有效性的明确结论。然而,长期随访和HPV-16/18/31/35感染的持续缺失为观察到的结果提供了中等程度的信心。

Long-Term Follow-Up After Surgical Treatment of Anogenital and Oropharyngeal Condyloma Acuminata with Simultaneous HPV Vaccination Initiation in Patients with Concurrent HPV-16 or -18 Co-Infection

Authors: Joanna Wojciula, Marcin Bartoszewicz, Magdalena Wojciula, Piotr Sienkiewicz, Grzegorz Szewczyk, Piotr Fiedor
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: Persistent infection with high-risk HPV genotypes accounts for the development of a substantial portion of anogenital and oropharyngeal malignancies. Patients with primary and secondary immunodeficiency or those receiving immunosuppressive therapy, including transplant recipients and patients affected by rare diseases, are vulnerable to particularly severe clinical manifestations of infection. While HPV vaccination constitutes primary prevention, patients with HPV-associated disease remain at risk of reinfection or subsequent infection with alternative genotypes following surgical treatment and may benefit from peri-operative vaccination regimens. This study aimed to evaluate long-term outcomes of HPV vaccination following surgical management of condyloma acuminata in patients with concurrent high-risk genotype infection. Methods: 350 patients with condyloma acuminata and HPV-16 or -18 co-infection confirmed by histopathological examination and PCR genotyping (oral cavity, vulva, vagina, penis, anoderma), including a subgroup with primary or acquired immunodeficiency or chronic immunosuppressive regimens, underwent surgical treatment, receiving simultaneous immunization with the first dose (subsequent completion of full regimen over 12 months) of the bivalent Cervarix vaccine (170 patients) or the quadrivalent Gardasil vaccine (180 patients). A follow-up examination, including HPV genotyping (16/18/31/35), was performed after 6 and 12 months following regimen completion. After 36 months, a standard clinical examination was performed, and in doubtful cases, supplemented with genotyping and biopsy/cytology. Observation was continued in subsequent years (5, 10, and >15 years). Results: Condyloma acuminata requiring removal were observed in 5% of the patients in follow-up at 36 months, with no significant statistical difference between vaccine groups. No cellular atypia or malignant transformation in the form of squamous cell carcinoma was observed. Genotyping did not detect HPV-16/18/31/35 infection in the follow-up examinations. Conclusions: In this uncontrolled, single-arm cohort, peri-operative initiation of either the bivalent or quadrivalent HPV vaccine was associated with a low rate of clinically apparent condyloma acuminata recurrence and no detectable HPV-16/18/31/35 infection during follow-up.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Persistent infection with high-risk HPV genotypes accounts for the development of a substantial portion of anogenital and oropharyngeal malignancies.
Design and population
350 patients with condyloma acuminata and HPV-16 or -18 co-infection confirmed by histopathological examination and PCR genotyping underwent surgical treatment, receiving simultaneous immunization with the first dose of the bivalent Cervarix vaccine or the quadrivalent Gardasil vaccine.
Core methods
Patients received either the bivalent Cervarix vaccine (170 patients) or the quadrivalent Gardasil vaccine (180 patients) during surgical treatment, with subsequent completion of the full regimen over 12 months. Follow-up examinations, including HPV genotyping (16/18/31/35), were conducted after 6 and 12 months, and a standard clinical examination was performed after 36 months, supplemented with genotyping and biopsy/cytology in doubtful cases.
Main results
Condyloma acuminata requiring removal were observed in 5% of the patients in follow-up at 36 months, with no significant statistical difference between vaccine groups. No cellular atypia or malignant transformation in the form of squamous cell carcinoma was observed. Genotyping did not detect HPV-16/18/31/35 infection in the follow-up examinations.
Interpretation and novelty
In this uncontrolled, single-arm cohort, peri-operative initiation of either the bivalent or quadrivalent HPV vaccine was associated with a low rate of clinically apparent condyloma acuminata recurrence and no detectable HPV-16/18/31/35 infection during follow-up.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that initiating HPV vaccination peri-operatively may reduce the risk of condyloma acuminata recurrence and prevent subsequent infection with HPV-16, -18, -31, or -35 in patients with concurrent high-risk genotype infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is uncontrolled and single-arm, which limits the ability to draw definitive conclusions about the efficacy of peri-operative vaccination. However, the long-term follow-up and consistent absence of HPV-16/18/31/35 infection provide moderate confidence in the observed outcomes.

人乳头瘤病毒感染对埃及转诊中心宫颈癌结局的预后影响-回顾性队列研究

Prognostic Impact of Human Papilloma Virus Infection on Cervical Cancer Outcome in Egyptian Referral Center- Retrospective Cohort Study
作者： Hayat Sharaf, Ahmed Sewidan, Alaa Elzarka
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 宫颈癌 仍是全球主要健康挑战。虽然 p16^INK4a^ (p16) 已被确立为 高危型HPV 相关致癌的诊断替代标志物,但其在侵袭性 宫颈癌 中的预后价值仍有争议。本研究评估了 p16 表达结合 2018 年 FIGO 分期的预后意义。方法 在单一三级转诊中心开展回顾性 队列研究 研究,纳入 2020 年 1 月至 2024 年 12 月期间接受治疗的 122 例组织学确诊的宫颈癌患者。评估流行病学和临床病理特征,特别关注 2018 年 FIGO 分期和 p16 免疫组织化学表达。采用 Kaplan–Meier 生存分析和 Cox 比例风险回归分析总体生存 (OS) 和无病生存 (DFS)。结果 在 122 例患者中,p16 表达与组织学亚型显著相关(p 55 年龄和 FIGO IV 期作为较差生存和复发增加的独立预测因素)。结论 在 宫颈癌 中,p16 表达不是独立的预后标志物。FIGO 分期和患者年龄是生存和复发的主要预测因素。尽管 p16 仍是肿瘤分类的有价值生物标志物,但仍需更大规模的前瞻性研究以进一步明确其预后价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒感染仍然是一个重大的全球健康挑战。尽管p16^INK4a^(p16)是人乳头瘤病毒相关致癌作用的一个公认的诊断替代标志物,但其在侵袭性人乳头瘤病毒感染中的预后价值仍存在争议。
设计与对象
本研究评估了p16表达与2018年FIGO分期联合的预后意义。在单个三级转诊中心进行了一项回顾性队列研究,包括2020年1月至2024年12月期间经组织学确诊的122例宫颈癌患者。
核心方法
评估了流行病学和病理学特征,特别是2018年FIGO分期和p16免疫组化表达。使用Kaplan–Meier生存分析和Cox比例风险回归分析总体生存率(OS)和无病生存率(DFS)。
主要结果
在122例患者中,p16表达与组织学亚型显著相关。年龄≥55岁和FIGO分期IV被识别为独立预测较差生存和增加复发的指标。
解释与创新
作者得出结论,p16表达不是一个独立的预后标志物。相反,FIGO分期和患者年龄是生存和复发的主要预测因素。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,在评估人乳头瘤病毒感染患者的预后时,应优先考虑FIGO分期和患者年龄。进一步的研究需要更好地定义p16在人乳头瘤病毒相关人乳头瘤病毒感染中的预后价值。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到回顾性设计和单中心设置的限制。证据为中等,因为它基于一个单中心三级转诊中心,没有包括前瞻性数据。

Prognostic Impact of Human Papilloma Virus Infection on Cervical Cancer Outcome in Egyptian Referral Center- Retrospective Cohort Study

Authors: Hayat Sharaf, Ahmed Sewidan, Alaa Elzarka
Original abstract (English)
Abstract BACKGROUND Cervical cancer remains a major global health challenge. Although p16^INK4a^ (p16) is a well-established diagnostic surrogate marker for high-risk HPV-related carcinogenesis, its prognostic value in invasive cervical cancer remains controversial. This study evaluated the prognostic significance of p16 expression in combination with the 2018 FIGO stage. METHODS A retrospective cohort study was conducted at a single tertiary referral center, including 122 patients with histologically confirmed cervical carcinoma treated between January 2020 and December 2024. Epidemiological and clinicopathological characteristics, with particular emphasis on the 2018 FIGO stage and p16 immunohistochemical expression, were evaluated. Overall survival (OS) and disease-free survival (DFS) were analyzed using Kaplan–Meier survival analysis and Cox proportional hazards regression. RESULTS Among the 122 patients, p16 expression was significantly associated with histopathological subtype (p 55 years and FIGO stage IV as independent predictors of poorer survival and increased recurrence. CONCLUSION In cervical cancer, p16 expression was not an independent prognostic marker. Instead, FIGO stage and patient age were the primary predictors of survival and recurrence. Although p16 remains a valuable biomarker for tumor classification, larger prospective studies are needed to better define its prognostic utility.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer remains a major global health challenge. Although p16^INK4a^ (p16) is a well-established diagnostic surrogate marker for high-risk HPV-related carcinogenesis, its prognostic value in invasive cervical cancer remains controversial.
Design and population
This study evaluated the prognostic significance of p16 expression in combination with the 2018 FIGO stage. A retrospective cohort study was conducted at a single tertiary referral center, including 122 patients with histologically confirmed cervical carcinoma treated between January 2020 and December 2024.
Core methods
Epidemiological and clinicopathological characteristics, with particular emphasis on the 2018 FIGO stage and p16 immunohistochemical expression, were evaluated. Overall survival (OS) and disease-free survival (DFS) were analyzed using Kaplan–Meier survival analysis and Cox proportional hazards regression.
Main results
Among the 122 patients, p16 expression was significantly associated with histopathological subtype. Age ≥55 years and FIGO stage IV were identified as independent predictors of poorer survival and increased recurrence.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that p16 expression was not an independent prognostic marker. Instead, FIGO stage and patient age were the primary predictors of survival and recurrence.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that FIGO stage and patient age should be prioritized in assessing prognosis for cervical cancer patients. Further research is needed to better define the prognostic utility of p16 in HPV-associated cervical cancer.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and single-center setting. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a single tertiary referral center and does not include prospective data.

病例报告: 在ALA-PDT清除CIN3后孤立持续HPV16感染可能引发空间上不同的原发性侵袭性宫颈癌

Case Report: Possible spatially-distinct de novo invasive cervical carcinoma in the setting of isolated persistent HPV16 after ALA-PDT-mediated histological clearance of CIN3
作者： Huihui Cai, Yanci Che, Yutong Liu, Jing Wang, Tingting Ma, Rui Yao
摘要中文翻译背景 宫颈上皮内瘤变3级(CIN3)是一种高级别癌前病变,5-氨基乙酰丙酸介导的光动力疗法(ALA-PDT)为年轻患者提供了一种保留生育能力的替代治疗方案。然而,完全病变消退并不能消除持续HPV16感染的潜在致癌风险,且在ALA-PDT治疗后长期失访(LTFU)情况下出现侵袭性癌变的现实证据仍较为有限。本病例报告旨在分享这一临床观察,并提供现实证据支持对孤立持续HPV16感染患者在组织学CIN3缓解后进行风险分层强化监测的策略。病例报告 一位32岁经产妇女,其宫颈9点和12点位置存在TZ2 CIN3,经过两次标准化ALA-PDT治疗后达到组织学缓解,但保留了孤立的HPV16感染。尽管多次临床提醒,她仍连续20个月停止所有监测。在自愿复诊时,发现新发的侵袭性鳞状细胞癌位于6点位置——这一宫颈象限与原发CIN3病变空间上不同。这一发现提示可能由持续HPV16感染引发新的恶性转化;然而,在缺乏病毒克隆性或HPV整合位点的分子证据情况下,不能排除既往隐匿性多灶性病变的可能性。多模式分期显示该患者理论上符合保留生育能力的根治性宫颈切除术的条件。结论 尽管癌前病变已缓解,孤立持续HPV16感染仍具有长期恶性潜能,并可能引发空间上不同的侵袭性癌变。由于该推论基于单一观察性病例且缺乏支持性分子数据,因此对孤立持续HPV16感染患者进行风险分层强化监测的策略仍需在更大规模的队列研究中进一步验证。需要标准化的随访依从性干预措施,以防止因间断监测导致的不可逆生育能力丧失和疾病进展。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究探讨了在ALA-PDT清除宫颈上皮内瘤变3级(CIN3)后,持续HPV16感染引发恶性转化的风险,特别是在年轻患者中的情况。
设计与对象
本研究是一项病例报告,研究对象为一位32岁经产妇女,其TZ2 CIN3在两次ALA-PDT治疗后达到组织学缓解,但保留了孤立的HPV16感染。她在停止监测连续20个月后,因新发侵袭性鳞状细胞癌出现在空间上不同的宫颈象限而复诊。
核心方法
该病例通过多模式分期评估其是否符合保留生育能力的根治性宫颈切除术的条件。回顾了患者的病史、治疗方案及随访依从性,并通过临床和组织学评估确认了HPV16的存在。
主要结果
复诊时确认在宫颈6点位置出现侵袭性鳞状细胞癌,该位置与原发CIN3病变空间上不同。患者存在孤立的HPV16感染,并已连续20个月停止所有监测。
解释与创新
作者认为,侵袭性癌变可能源于持续HPV16感染的原发性恶性转化,但缺乏病毒克隆性或整合位点的分子证据时,不能排除既往隐匿性多灶性病变的可能性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果突显了孤立持续HPV16感染可能带来的长期恶性风险,即使在CIN3缓解后仍需持续进行监测,以防止疾病进展和生育能力丧失。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于单一观察性病例,缺乏支持性分子数据,因此限制了推论的强度。由于缺乏更大规模队列研究的验证,证据强度被评估为中等。

Case Report: Possible spatially-distinct de novo invasive cervical carcinoma in the setting of isolated persistent HPV16 after ALA-PDT-mediated histological clearance of CIN3

Authors: Huihui Cai, Yanci Che, Yutong Liu, Jing Wang, Tingting Ma, Rui Yao
Original abstract (English)
Background Cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 3 (CIN3) is a high-grade precancer, and 5-aminolaevulinic acid-mediated photodynamic therapy (ALA-PDT) offers a fertility-preserving alternative for young patients. However, complete lesion regression does not eliminate the latent oncogenic risk of persistent HPV16, and real-world evidence of invasive carcinoma arising after long-term loss to follow-up (LTFU) post-PDT remains scarce. This case report aims to share this clinical observation and provide real-world evidence supporting risk-stratified post-treatment surveillance for patients with isolated persistent HPV16 after histological CIN3 remission. Case presentation A 32-year-old parous woman with TZ2 CIN3 at the 9/12 o'clock cervical positions achieved histological remission after two cycles of standardized ALA-PDT, yet retained isolated HPV16 infection. Despite repeated clinical reminders, she discontinued all surveillance for 20 consecutive months. Upon voluntary re-presentation, newly developed invasive squamous cell carcinoma was confirmed at the 6 o'clock position—a cervical quadrant spatially distinct from the original CIN3 lesions. This finding raises the possibility of de novo malignant transformation from persistent HPV16; however, pre-existing occult multifocal disease cannot be excluded in the absence of viral clonality or HPV integration-site molecular evidence. Multimodal staging indicated theoretical eligibility for fertility-sparing radical trachelectomy. Conclusions Isolated persistent HPV16 retains long-term malignant potential despite resolved precancerous lesions, and can drive spatially separate invasive carcinoma. Because this inference is based on a single observational case without supporting molecular data, risk-stratified intensified surveillance should be further validated in larger cohorts. Standardized follow-up adherence interventions are required to prevent irreversible fertility loss and advanced disease secondary to discontinuous monitoring.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study addresses the risk of malignant transformation following the resolution of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 3 (CIN3) with ALA-PDT, particularly in the context of persistent HPV16 infection.
Design and population
The study is a case report of a 32-year-old parous woman with TZ2 CIN3 who achieved histological remission after two cycles of ALA-PDT but retained isolated HPV16 infection. She discontinued surveillance for 20 consecutive months before presenting with newly developed invasive squamous cell carcinoma in a spatially distinct cervical quadrant.
Core methods
The case was evaluated using multimodal staging to assess eligibility for fertility-sparing radical trachelectomy. The patient's medical history, treatment protocol, and follow-up adherence were reviewed, and the presence of HPV16 was confirmed through clinical and histological assessments.
Main results
Upon re-presentation, invasive squamous cell carcinoma was confirmed at the 6 o'clock cervical position, which was spatially distinct from the original CIN3 lesions. The patient had isolated HPV16 infection and had discontinued surveillance for 20 months.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors suggest that the invasive carcinoma may have arisen de novo from persistent HPV16 infection, although pre-existing occult multifocal disease cannot be ruled out without molecular evidence of viral clonality or integration sites.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the potential long-term malignant risk associated with isolated persistent HPV16 infection, even after the resolution of CIN3, and underscore the need for continued surveillance to prevent advanced disease and fertility loss.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a single observational case without supporting molecular data, which limits the strength of the inference. The evidence is considered moderate due to the lack of broader validation in larger cohorts.

塔吉克斯坦对10-14岁女孩进行人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)大规模疫苗接种的第一项结果

First Results of Mass Immunization of Girls Against Human Papillomavirus in Tajikistan
作者： J. A. Abdullozoda, Г М Ходжамурадов, L. А. Ashrafyan, F. B. Saidzoda, Akbar Ismatullo Murodzoda, N. A. Abdugaffarova, M. S. Bobokhonova, T. Sh. Saimuddinov, Р А Турсунов, G. M. Muhsinzoda
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估在塔吉克斯坦针对10-14岁女孩开展的人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗接种运动的第一项结果,考虑到该国宫颈癌的发病率正在上升。
设计与对象
该研究分析了共和国免疫预防中心2014年至2025年HPV免疫进展情况以及塔吉克斯坦发病率的统计数据和报告。
核心方法
本研究利用来自多个专门医疗机构的统计数据和报告,评估了塔吉克斯坦的人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗接种进展以及宫颈癌的发病率。
主要结果
过去五年(2021年至2025年),塔吉克斯坦宫颈癌的发病率增长了1.5倍,2025年新增病例434例。10-14岁女孩的疫苗接种覆盖率达到95.4%,记录不良反应48例,占0.009%。
解释与创新
该研究将 发病率 的增加解释为由于视觉筛查覆盖范围的扩大以及可能的实际 发病率 的增加。疫苗接种 的高覆盖率和低不良反应率被认为是积极的结果。
科研与公卫意义
该研究的结果为实现国家和国际目标奠定了基础,以消除 宫颈癌 并改善塔吉克斯坦妇女的生殖和公共卫生。
局限与证据强度
由于研究的观察性质以及缺乏详细的方法学描述,本研究的证据强度为中等。

First Results of Mass Immunization of Girls Against Human Papillomavirus in Tajikistan

Authors: J. A. Abdullozoda, Г М Ходжамурадов, L. А. Ashrafyan, F. B. Saidzoda, Akbar Ismatullo Murodzoda, N. A. Abdugaffarova, M. S. Bobokhonova, T. Sh. Saimuddinov, Р А Турсунов, G. M. Muhsinzoda
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) causes 4.5 % of all new cancer cases worldwide, including cervical cancer. Cervical cancer accounts for the vast majority (83 %) of HPV-associated cancers. To evaluate the first results of the immunization campaign against human papillomavirus in girls aged 10–14 years in the context of increasing incidence of cervical cancer in the Republic of Tajikistan. Materials and Methods. The study analyzed statistical data and reports from the Republican Center for Immunoprophylaxis on the progress of HPV immunization in the republic, as well as on the incidence of cervical cancer, obtained from various specialized medical institutions for the period 2014–2025. Results and Discussion . In 2025, 434 new cases of cervical cancer were registered in the Republic of Tajikistan, with a prevalence rate of 4.1 cases per 100,000 women. Over the past five years (2021 to 2025), this rate has increased by 1.5 times. This annual increase is due to the expansion of visual screening coverage under the state program, as well as a possible increase in actual incidence. Since July 2025, the Republic of Tajikistan has become the 53rd country in the WHO European Region to introduce HPV vaccination into its National Immunization Program. The government officially announced the launch of an HPV vaccination campaign for girls aged 10–14 years on October 1, 2025. The preferred vaccination regimen for Tajikistan is the single-dose quadrivalent Gardasil® vaccine, manufactured by Merck & Co. Thanks to collaboration with GAVI, Tajikistan received 623,000 doses of this vaccine. As of October 31, 2025, the number of girls aged 10–14 vaccinated nationwide reached 95.4 % of the target population, or 522,134 girls. The range of vaccination coverage across all regions of the country ranged from 94.9 % to 96.9 %. During the specified period, 48 cases of adverse reactions after immunization against HPV were registered, which amounted to 0.009 %. The most common symptoms were: weakness/malaise (n = 15; 31.25 %); redness of the skin/face (n = 10; 20.8 %); nausea (n = 8; 16.7 %); fainting (n = 7; 14.6 %); increased body temperature (n = 7; 14.6 %) and collapse (n = 5; 10.4 %). Introduction routine HPV vaccination in the National Immunization Schedule of the Republic of Tajikistan is a prudent and strategically important step. Implementing these measures creates the foundation for achieving national and international goals to eliminate cervical cancer and improve the reproductive and public health of women in the country.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the first results of the immunization campaign against human papillomavirus (HPV) in girls aged 10–14 years in Tajikistan, given the increasing incidence of cervical cancer in the country.
Design and population
The study analyzed statistical data and reports from the Republican Center for Immunoprophylaxis on the progress of HPV immunization and the incidence of cervical cancer in Tajikistan from 2014 to 2025.
Core methods
The study utilized statistical data and reports from various specialized medical institutions to assess the progress of HPV immunization and the incidence of cervical cancer in Tajikistan.
Main results
Over the past five years (2021 to 2025), the incidence of cervical cancer in Tajikistan increased by 1.5 times, with 434 new cases registered in 2025. The vaccination coverage among girls aged 10–14 years reached 95.4%, with 48 cases of adverse reactions recorded, amounting to 0.009%.
Interpretation and novelty
The study interpreted the increased incidence of cervical cancer as due to the expansion of visual screening coverage and possible actual incidence increase. The high vaccination coverage and low adverse reaction rate were noted as positive outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study's findings create a foundation for achieving national and international goals to eliminate cervical cancer and improve women's reproductive and public health in Tajikistan.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature and the reliance on statistical data without detailed methodological descriptions.
