评估真实世界 HPV16 和 HPV18 特异性 疫苗 免疫监测的高通量替代检测:PBNA、cCLIA 和 IgG 的比较

Assessing high-throughput surrogate assays for real-world HPV16- and HPV18-specific vaccine immune monitoring: a comparison of PBNA, cCLIA, and IgG
作者： Weiya Mao, Meng Yuan, Zhaoyang Fang, Renyun Zha, Yuxiang Ye, Lin Cai, Linlin Wang, Ruopan Huang, Shuhong Luo, Cheng Guo
摘要中文翻译简介 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 后的 免疫应答 的实际评估需要血清学监测,但基于伪病毒粒子的中和测定 (PBNA) 难以大规模应用。方法 本研究比较了 PBNA、竞争性化学发光免疫分析 (cCLIA) 和基于病毒样颗粒的间接 IgG 免疫分析,以评估 HPV16 和 HPV18 特异性抗体。横断面成分包括完全接种疫苗的接受者和未接种疫苗的对照,而纵向成分包括接受两剂二价 HPV 疫苗 的青春期女孩。评估了相关性、分类性能、系统偏差和一致性。结果 所有三种检测均反映了 HPV16/HPV18 特异性 疫苗 诱导的抗体反应趋势。与 IgG 测定相比,cCLIA 与 PBNA 结果的关系更加稳定,尤其是在纵向监测和第二次给药后。尽管对于 PBNA 定义的功能分类,IgG 检测的稳定性低于 cCLIA,但它始终如一地捕获了 疫苗 在群体水平上诱导的抗体反应的总体模式。持续的数值差异表明高通量测定不能与中和滴度直接互换。讨论 这些发现支持 HPV16 和 HPV18 特异性免疫监测的分层策略,使用 PBNA 作为功能实验室参考,cCLIA 用于高通量替代评估,以及 IgG 测试用于人群水平趋势监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估高通量替代检测在现实世界 HPV16 和 HPV18 特异性免疫监测中的效用,因为血清学监测对于评估 免疫应答 HPV 疫苗接种 后至关重要,但 PBNA 大规模实施具有挑战性。
设计与对象
该研究包括全面疫苗接种者和未接种疫苗的对照的横断面部分,以及追踪接受两剂二价 HPV 疫苗 的青春期女孩的纵向部分。
核心方法
该研究比较了 PBNA、竞争性化学发光免疫分析 (cCLIA) 和基于病毒样颗粒的间接 IgG 免疫分析对 HPV16 和 HPV18 特异性抗体的评估,评估相关性、分类性能、系统偏倚和一致性。
主要结果
所有三种检测均反映了 HPV16/HPV18 特异性 疫苗 诱导的抗体反应趋势,与 IgG 检测相比,cCLIA 显示与 PBNA 结果的关系更稳定,特别是在纵向监测和第二次给药后。
解释与创新
研究结果建议采用分层方法进行 HPV16 和 HPV18 特异性免疫监测,使用 PBNA 作为功能参考,使用 cCLIA 进行高通量评估,使用 IgG 检测进行人群水平趋势监测。
科研与公卫意义
结果为 HPV16 和 HPV18 特异性免疫监测的不同检测方法的实际应用提供了见解,这可以为公共卫生领域的 监测 和人群水平趋势分析提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横断面和纵向部分可能限制其推广性,且持续的数值差异表明高通量检测方法不能直接替代中和抗体滴度,表明证据强度中等。

Assessing high-throughput surrogate assays for real-world HPV16- and HPV18-specific vaccine immune monitoring: a comparison of PBNA, cCLIA, and IgG

Authors: Weiya Mao, Meng Yuan, Zhaoyang Fang, Renyun Zha, Yuxiang Ye, Lin Cai, Linlin Wang, Ruopan Huang, Shuhong Luo, Cheng Guo
Original abstract (English)
Introduction Serological monitoring is needed for the real-world evaluation of immune responses after human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination, but the pseudovirion-based neutralization assay (PBNA) is difficult to apply at scale. Methods This study compared PBNA, competitive chemiluminescent immunoassay (cCLIA), and a virus-like particle-based indirect IgG immunoassay for HPV16- and HPV18-specific antibody assessment. The cross-sectional component included fully vaccinated recipients and unvaccinated controls, while the longitudinal component included adolescent girls receiving a two-dose bivalent HPV vaccine. Correlation, classification performance, systematic bias, and agreement were evaluated. Results All three assays reflected HPV16/HPV18-specific vaccine-induced antibody response trends. cCLIA showed a more stable relationship with PBNA results than the IgG assay, especially in longitudinal monitoring and after the second dose. Although the IgG assay showed lower stability than cCLIA for PBNA-defined functional classification, it consistently captured the overall pattern of vaccine-induced antibody responses at the population level. Persistent numerical differences indicated that the high-throughput assays were not directly interchangeable with neutralization titers. Discussion These findings support a stratified strategy for HPV16- and HPV18-specific immune monitoring using PBNA as a functional laboratory reference, cCLIA for high-throughput surrogate assessment, and IgG testing for population-level trend monitoring.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the utility of high-throughput surrogate assays for real-world HPV16- and HPV18-specific immune monitoring, as serological monitoring is essential for assessing immune responses post-HPV vaccination, but PBNA is challenging to implement at scale.
Design and population
The study included a cross-sectional component with fully vaccinated recipients and unvaccinated controls, as well as a longitudinal component tracking adolescent girls receiving a two-dose bivalent HPV vaccine.
Core methods
The study compared PBNA, competitive chemiluminescent immunoassay (cCLIA), and a virus-like particle-based indirect IgG immunoassay for HPV16- and HPV18-specific antibody assessment, evaluating correlation, classification performance, systematic bias, and agreement.
Main results
All three assays reflected HPV16/HPV18-specific vaccine-induced antibody response trends, with cCLIA showing a more stable relationship with PBNA results than the IgG assay, especially in longitudinal monitoring and after the second dose.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest a stratified approach for HPV16- and HPV18-specific immune monitoring, using PBNA as a functional reference, cCLIA for high-throughput assessment, and IgG testing for population-level trend monitoring.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the practical application of different assays for HPV16- and HPV18-specific immune monitoring, which can inform surveillance and population-level trend analysis in public health.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's cross-sectional and longitudinal components may limit generalizability, and persistent numerical differences suggest that high-throughput assays are not directly interchangeable with neutralization titers, indicating moderate evidence strength.

知识、态度和对疫苗接种疫苗的接受度:东南尼日利亚某三级医院生殖年龄女性的调查

Knowledge, Attitudes, and Acceptance of Human Papillomavirus Vaccination Among Women of Reproductive Age Attending a Tertiary Hospital in Southeast Nigeria
作者： Emmanuel M. Akwuruoha, David O. Akwuruoha, Vanessa A. Chigbundu, Augustine I. Airaodion
摘要中文翻译背景:人乳头瘤病毒(人乳头瘤病毒,HPV)感染是导致宫颈癌(宫颈癌)的主要原因之一,尤其是在低收入和中等收入国家,由于预防性服务获取有限,这一问题更为突出。尽管HPV 疫苗接种疫苗已被证明能有效预防与HPV相关的疾病,但在许多发展中国家,疫苗接种率仍较低。目的:本研究评估了东南尼日利亚某三级医院就诊的生殖年龄女性对HPV 疫苗接种疫苗的知识、态度和接受度。方法:在尼日利亚阿巴市阿比亚州大学教学医院就诊的366名15至49岁女性中开展了一项描述性横断面研究。采用多阶段抽样方法选取参与者。通过结构化的、由访谈员实施的问卷收集数据,评估社会人口学特征、对HPV及其疫苗的认知、对疫苗接种疫苗的态度以及对疫苗疫苗的接受度。数据使用IBM SPSS Statistics版本26.0进行分析,统计学显著性设定为p < 0.05。结果:大多数受访者年龄在25至34岁之间(40.17%),已婚(52.19%),拥有高等教育(50.27%)。76.78%的参与者报告了对HPV的知晓。总体而言,45.63%的受访者对HPV 疫苗接种疫苗有良好的认知,37.43%有中等认知,16.94%认知较差。社交媒体(47.27%)和互联网(34.43%)是主要的信息来源。63.39%的受访者表现出对HPV 疫苗接种疫苗的积极态度,而59.29%的受访者表现出较高的疫苗疫苗接受度。知识、态度和接受度的分布具有统计学显著性(p < 0.001)。结论:在三级医院就诊的女性表现出相对较高的知识水平、积极的态度和较高的疫苗接种疫苗接受度。然而,关于疫苗疫苗安全性、剂量安排和潜在副作用的重要知识缺口仍然存在。加强健康教育并扩大医疗保健提供者和数字健康干预措施,可能进一步提高东南尼日利亚的疫苗疫苗接种率。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染是 宫颈癌 的主要原因,特别是在获得预防服务有限的低收入和中等收入国家。
设计与对象
在尼日利亚阿巴市阿比亚州大学教学医院就诊的366名15至49岁女性中开展了一项描述性横断面研究。
核心方法
数据通过结构化的、由访谈员实施的问卷收集,评估社会人口学特征、对HPV及其疫苗的认知、对疫苗接种疫苗的态度以及对疫苗疫苗的接受度。数据使用IBM SPSS Statistics版本26.0进行分析,统计学显著性设定为p < 0.05。
主要结果
大多数受访者年龄在25至34岁之间(40.17%),已婚(52.19%),拥有高等教育(50.27%)。76.78%的参与者报告了对HPV的知晓。总体而言,45.63%的受访者对HPV 疫苗接种疫苗有良好的认知,37.43%有中等认知,16.94%认知较差。知识、态度和接受度的分布具有统计学显著性(p < 0.001)。
解释与创新
在三级医院就诊的女性表现出相对较高的知识水平、积极的态度和较高的疫苗接种疫苗接受度。然而,关于疫苗疫苗安全性、剂量安排和潜在副作用的重要知识缺口仍然存在。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,加强健康教育、扩大医疗保健提供者和数字健康干预措施可能会进一步提高尼日利亚东南部的 疫苗 的使用率。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于东南尼日利亚的一家三级医院,这可能影响研究结果的普遍性。由于横断面研究设计和可能存在自我报告偏差,证据强度中等。

Knowledge, Attitudes, and Acceptance of Human Papillomavirus Vaccination Among Women of Reproductive Age Attending a Tertiary Hospital in Southeast Nigeria

Authors: Emmanuel M. Akwuruoha, David O. Akwuruoha, Vanessa A. Chigbundu, Augustine I. Airaodion
Original abstract (English)
Background: Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is a major cause of cervical cancer, particularly in low- and middle-income countries where access to preventive services is limited. Despite the proven effectiveness of HPV vaccination in preventing HPV-related diseases, vaccine uptake remains low in many developing countries. Aims: This study assessed the knowledge, attitudes, and acceptance of HPV vaccination among women of reproductive age attending a tertiary hospital in Southeast Nigeria. Methods: A descriptive cross-sectional study was conducted among 366 women aged 15 to 49 years attending Abia State University Teaching Hospital, Aba, Nigeria. Participants were selected through a multistage sampling technique. Data were collected using a structured, interviewer-administered questionnaire that assessed sociodemographic characteristics, knowledge of HPV and HPV vaccination, attitudes towards vaccination, and vaccine acceptance. Data were analysed using IBM SPSS Statistics version 26.0, with statistical significance set at p < 0.05. Results: Most respondents were 25 to 34 years old (40.17%), married (52.19%), and had tertiary education (50.27%). Awareness of HPV was reported by 76.78% of participants. Overall, 45.63% demonstrated good knowledge, 37.43% had moderate knowledge, and 16.94% had poor knowledge of HPV vaccination. Social media (47.27%) and the internet (34.43%) were the main sources of information. Positive attitudes towards HPV vaccination were reported by 63.39% of respondents, while 59.29% demonstrated high vaccine acceptance. The distributions of knowledge, attitudes, and acceptance were statistically significant (p < 0.001). Conclusion: Women attending the tertiary hospital demonstrated relatively high levels of knowledge, positive attitudes, and substantial acceptance of HPV vaccination. However, important knowledge gaps regarding vaccine safety, dosage schedules, and potential side effects remain. Strengthening health education and expanding healthcare provider and digital health interventions may further improve vaccine uptake in Southeast Nigeria.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is a major cause of cervical cancer, particularly in low- and middle-income countries where access to preventive services is limited.
Design and population
A descriptive cross-sectional study was conducted among 366 women aged 15 to 49 years attending Abia State University Teaching Hospital, Aba, Nigeria.
Core methods
Data were collected using a structured, interviewer-administered questionnaire that assessed sociodemographic characteristics, knowledge of HPV and HPV vaccination, attitudes towards vaccination, and vaccine acceptance. Data were analysed using IBM SPSS Statistics version 26.0, with statistical significance set at p < 0.05.
Main results
Most respondents were 25 to 34 years old (40.17%), married (52.19%), and had tertiary education (50.27%). Awareness of HPV was reported by 76.78% of participants. Overall, 45.63% demonstrated good knowledge, 37.43% had moderate knowledge, and 16.94% had poor knowledge of HPV vaccination. The distributions of knowledge, attitudes, and acceptance were statistically significant (p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
Women attending the tertiary hospital demonstrated relatively high levels of knowledge, positive attitudes, and substantial acceptance of HPV vaccination. However, important knowledge gaps regarding vaccine safety, dosage schedules, and potential side effects remain.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that strengthening health education and expanding healthcare provider and digital health interventions may further improve vaccine uptake in Southeast Nigeria.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single tertiary hospital in Southeast Nigeria, which may affect the generalisability of the results. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the cross-sectional design and the potential for self-reporting bias.

人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 尼日利亚阿巴州阿比亚州立大学教学医院的女性接受治疗和感知到的障碍

Human Papillomavirus Vaccination Uptake and Perceived Barriers among Women Attending Abia State University Teaching Hospital, Aba, Nigeria
作者： Emmanuel M. Akwuruoha, David O. Akwuruoha, Vanessa A. Chigbundu, Augustine I. Airaodion
摘要中文翻译背景:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 仍然是预防 宫颈癌 的最有效策略之一。然而,由于社会、经济和卫生系统的挑战,许多低收入和中等收入国家的女性对 疫苗 的吸收仍然不够理想。目的:本研究评估了在尼日利亚阿巴阿比亚州立大学教学医院 (ABSUTH) 就诊的女性中 HPV 疫苗接种 的摄取情况和感知到的障碍。方法:对 366 名就读阿坝 ABSUTH 的 15 至 49 岁女性进行了一项以医院为基础的描述性横断面研究。参与者是使用根据世界卫生组织行为和社会驱动因素框架开发的结构化、访谈员管理的调查问卷来选择的。使用 IBM SPSS Statistics 26.0 版收集和分析有关社会人口特征、HPV 疫苗接种 历史、感知障碍、社会影响、可及性以及提高 疫苗 吸收策略的数据。使用描述性统计、平均分、百分比和卡方检验进行数据分析,统计显着性设定为p < 0.05。结果:只有 25.68% 的受访者曾接种过 HPV 疫苗,而 74.32% 的受访者从未接种过疫苗。两种 疫苗 剂量是最常报告的方案 (44.68%)。疫苗接种 最重要的障碍是高成本(74.86%;平均值 = 3.97)、担心副作用(67.49%;平均值 = 3.72)、疫苗 可用性有限(63.66%;平均值 = 3.62)、信息不足(61.20%;平均值 = 3.54)以及医护人员讨论不足(60.93%;平均值 = 3.62) 3.50)。政府补贴(86.61%)、免费疫苗接种(84.43%)、宣传活动(83.33%)和提高疫苗可用性(82.79%)是疫苗吸收的最强促进因素。疫苗接种 的吸收与医护人员讨论(χ2 = 3.945,p = 0.047)、医护人员推荐(χ2 = 21.158,p < 0.001)和同伴影响(χ2 = 47.607,p < 0.001)之间观察到显着相关性。结论:参加 ABSUTH 的女性中 HPV 疫苗接种 的摄取率较低。财务限制、准入有限、信息不足以及提供者沟通不畅是主要障碍。扩大公众意识、补贴 疫苗接种 以及加强医护人员的参与可能会大大提高 HPV 疫苗 的吸收率。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估尼日利亚女性中 HPV 疫苗接种 的吸收率和感知到的障碍,解决提高 疫苗 覆盖率的需要,这些国家的 疫苗 的覆盖率仍不理想。
设计与对象
对尼日利亚阿巴阿比亚州立大学教学医院的 366 名 15 至 49 岁女性进行了一项以医院为基础的描述性横断面研究。
核心方法
参与者是使用根据世界卫生组织行为和社会驱动因素框架开发的结构化、访谈员管理的调查问卷来选择的。使用描述性统计、平均分、百分比和卡方检验进行数据分析,统计显着性设定为p < 0.05。
主要结果
只有 25.68% 的受访者曾接受过 HPV 疫苗,成本高、担心副作用、疫苗 可用性有限、信息不足和医护人员讨论不足是最重要的障碍。
解释与创新
研究发现,经济拮据、获取机会有限、信息不足和提供者沟通不畅是尼日利亚女性感染 HPV 疫苗 的主要障碍。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,扩大公众意识、补贴 疫苗接种 以及加强医护人员的参与可能会大大提高类似环境中 HPV 疫苗 的吸收率。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横断面设计和样本量的限制,横断面设计没有建立因果关系,样本量可能无法代表更广泛的人群。

Human Papillomavirus Vaccination Uptake and Perceived Barriers among Women Attending Abia State University Teaching Hospital, Aba, Nigeria

Authors: Emmanuel M. Akwuruoha, David O. Akwuruoha, Vanessa A. Chigbundu, Augustine I. Airaodion
Original abstract (English)
Background: Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination remains one of the most effective strategies for preventing cervical cancer. However, vaccine uptake among women in many low- and middle-income countries remains suboptimal due to social, economic, and health system challenges. Aim: This study assessed HPV vaccination uptake and perceived barriers among women attending Abia State University Teaching Hospital (ABSUTH), Aba, Nigeria. Methods: A hospital-based descriptive cross-sectional study was conducted among 366 women aged 15 to 49 years attending ABSUTH, Aba. Participants were selected using a structured, interviewer-administered questionnaire developed in accordance with the World Health Organisation behavioural and social drivers’ framework. Data on socio-demographic characteristics, HPV vaccination history, perceived barriers, social influences, accessibility, and strategies for improving vaccine uptake were collected and analysed using IBM SPSS Statistics version 26.0. Descriptive statistics, mean scores, percentages, and the chi-square test were used for data analysis, with statistical significance set at p < 0.05. Results: Only 25.68% of respondents had previously received the HPV vaccine, whereas 74.32% had never been vaccinated. Two vaccine doses were the most frequently reported schedule (44.68%). The most important barriers to vaccination were high cost (74.86%; mean = 3.97), fear of side effects (67.49%; mean = 3.72), limited vaccine availability (63.66%; mean = 3.62), inadequate information (61.20%; mean = 3.54), and insufficient healthcare worker discussion (60.93%; mean = 3.50). Government subsidy (86.61%), free vaccination (84.43%), awareness campaigns (83.33%), and improved vaccine availability (82.79%) were the strongest facilitators of vaccine uptake. Significant associations were observed between vaccination uptake and healthcare worker discussion (χ2 = 3.945, p = 0.047), healthcare worker recommendation (χ2 = 21.158, p < 0.001), and peer influence (χ2 = 47.607, p < 0.001). Conclusion: HPV vaccination uptake among women attending ABSUTH was low. Financial constraints, limited access, inadequate information, and poor provider communication were major barriers. Expanding public awareness, subsidising vaccination, and strengthening healthcare worker engagement may substantially improve HPV vaccine uptake.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess HPV vaccination uptake and perceived barriers among women in Nigeria, addressing the need to improve vaccine coverage in low- and middle-income countries where uptake remains suboptimal.
Design and population
A hospital-based descriptive cross-sectional study was conducted among 366 women aged 15 to 49 years attending Abia State University Teaching Hospital, Aba, Nigeria.
Core methods
Participants were selected using a structured, interviewer-administered questionnaire developed in accordance with the World Health Organisation behavioural and social drivers’ framework. Descriptive statistics, mean scores, percentages, and the chi-square test were used for data analysis, with statistical significance set at p < 0.05.
Main results
Only 25.68% of respondents had previously received the HPV vaccine, with high cost, fear of side effects, limited vaccine availability, inadequate information, and insufficient healthcare worker discussion being the most important barriers.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that financial constraints, limited access, inadequate information, and poor provider communication were major barriers to HPV vaccine uptake among women in Nigeria.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that expanding public awareness, subsidising vaccination, and strengthening healthcare worker engagement may substantially improve HPV vaccine uptake in similar settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which does not establish causality, and the sample size, which may not be representative of the broader population.

中国宫颈癌发病率与高危型人乳头瘤病毒感染相关的地理和时间变化:一项基于人群的研究

Geographic and temporal variations in cancer incidence attributable to human papillomavirus infection in China: a population-based study
作者： Junli Liu, Junrong Hu, Qixun Zhu, Runquan Chen, Junyao Ye, Yihuan Zheng, Feixue Wei, Ganfeng Luo, Yanting Zhang
摘要中文翻译目前中国发病率(HPV)感染引起的癌症发病率的流行病学数据仍不清楚。本研究对 2013 年至 2017 年中国 HPV 相关癌症 发病率 的程度和地理变异性、1988 年至 2017 年的时间趋势以及对 2032 年的预测进行了最新评估。数据提取自五大洲癌症 发病率 第 VII – XII 卷。通过将每个解剖部位的 高危型HPV 患病率 乘以 HPV 阳性病例中的特定生物标志物阳性率来计算人群归因分数。连接点回归和年龄周期 队列研究 模型分别用于量化时间年龄标准化 发病率 率 (ASR) 趋势以及年龄和出生 队列研究 效应。构建贝叶斯年龄周期 队列研究 模型来预测到 2032 年的 HPV 归因癌症 发病率。2013 年至 2017 年,估计中国女性和男性中分别观察到 68,955 例和 3463 例 HPV 归因癌症病例,ASR 为 11.20(95% 置信区间 [CI]:每 100,000 人为 10.98–11.41) 和 0.52 (0.20–0.82)。最常见的 HPV 相关癌症是 宫颈癌(女性;ASR [95% CI]:每 100,000 人中 10.92 [10.83–11.00])和喉癌(男性;每 100,000 人中 0.21 [0.03–0.37])。在纳入的 25 个省份中,HPV 归因的 宫颈癌 的 ASR 范围为北京每 10 万人 6.15(5.88-6.42)到湖南每 10 万人 17.43(15.84-19.03)(相差 2.8 倍)。多变量线性生态回归分析中,省人类发展指数与宫颈癌 发病率 呈负相关。从 1988 年到 2017 年,HPV 归因的 宫颈癌(女性;平均年百分比变化 [AAPC] 和 95% CI:5.89% [2.82–9.06])以及阴茎癌和口咽癌(男性;1.98% [0.82–3.15] 和 9.65%)的 ASR 有所增加分别为[5.81–13.62])。在 1908 年至 1978 年间出生的连续几代人中,观察到女性中 HPV 相关的 宫颈癌 和男女口咽癌的风险显着增加。根据 2017 年历史趋势的反事实延续,在不纳入后续 疫苗接种 或筛查扩展的情况下,HPV 归因的 宫颈癌 的 ASR 预计将在 2028 年至 2032 年达到每 10 万人 19.53(95% 不确定性区间 [ UI ]:9.99-34.36),而这一数字为 10.92 (95% CI:10.83–11.00) 2013–2017 年每 100,000 人。在中国,HPV 相关癌症的 发病率 根据癌症类型和地理位置的不同而有很大差异。这些发现凸显了对区域特异性 HPV 发病率 和癌症筛查策略的需求,特别是在 发病率 发病率高的地区。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
目前,中国与高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染相关的癌症发病率数据仍不清楚。本研究旨在通过分析HPV相关癌症的地理和时间变化来填补这一空白。
设计与对象
本研究在中国2013年至2017年间进行,分析了1988年至2017年的趋势,并预测到2032年。数据来自25个省份,重点关注女性和男性中的HPV相关癌症。
核心方法
使用Joinpoint回归和年龄-时期-出生队列模型来量化时间标准化发病率趋势以及年龄和出生队列效应。通过将每个解剖部位的发病率乘以HPV阳性病例中特定生物标志物的阳性率,计算了人群归因分数。
主要结果
An estimated 68,955 and 3,463 HPV-attributable cancer cases were observed among females and males in China in 2013–2017, respectively, with ASRs of 11.20 (95% confidence interval [CI]: 10.98–11.41) and 0.52 (0.20–0.82) per 100,000. From 1988 to 2017, the ASRs increased for HPV-attributable cervical cancer (females; average annual percent change [AAPC] and 95% CI: 5.89% [2.82–9.06]) and penile and oropharyngeal cancers (males; 1.98% [0.82–3.15] and 9.65% [5.81–13.62], respectively).
解释与创新
本研究突出了在1908年至1978年间出生的连续几代人群中,女性HPV相关宫颈癌和男女双方的口咽癌均显示出显著上升的风险。这些发现为理解中国HPV相关癌症的长期趋势和地理差异提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了在高发病率地区制定特定区域的HPV疫苗接种和癌症筛查策略的重要性,以应对中国HPV相关癌症日益增长的负担。
局限与证据强度
本研究依赖于《癌症在五大洲的发病率》第七至第十二卷的数据,这些数据可能存在质量或完整性方面的局限性。由于预测假设历史趋势持续,未考虑未来疫苗接种或筛查干预,因此证据强度中等。

Geographic and temporal variations in cancer incidence attributable to human papillomavirus infection in China: a population-based study

Authors: Junli Liu, Junrong Hu, Qixun Zhu, Runquan Chen, Junyao Ye, Yihuan Zheng, Feixue Wei, Ganfeng Luo, Yanting Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Current epidemiological data on cancer incidence attributable to human papillomavirus (HPV) infection in China remain unclear. This study provides an updated assessment of the magnitude of and geographic variability in the incidence of HPV-attributable cancer in China in 2013–2017, temporal trends from 1988 to 2017 and projections to 2032. Data were extracted from the Cancer Incidence in Five Continents Volumes VII – XII . The population-attributable fraction was calculated by multiplying the high-risk HPV prevalence at each anatomical site by the specific biomarker positivity rate in HPV-positive cases. Joinpoint regression and age-period-cohort models were used to quantify temporal age-standardized incidence rate (ASR) trends and age and birth cohort effects, respectively. Bayesian age-period-cohort models were constructed to project the HPV-attributable cancer incidence through 2032. An estimated 68,955 and 3463 HPV-attributable cancer cases were observed among females and males in China in 2013–2017, respectively, with ASRs of 11.20 (95% confidence interval [ CI ]: 10.98–11.41) and 0.52 (0.20–0.82) per 100,000. The most common HPV-attributable cancers were cervical cancer (females; ASR [95% CI ]: 10.92 [10.83–11.00] per 100,000) and laryngeal cancer (males; 0.21 [0.03–0.37] per 100,000). Among the 25 provinces included, the ASRs of HPV-attributable cervical cancer ranged from 6.15 (5.88–6.42) per 100,000 in Beijing to 17.43 (15.84–19.03) per 100,000 in Hunan (2.8-fold difference). The provincial Human Development Index was inversely associated with the cervical cancer incidence in multivariable linear ecological regression analyses. From 1988 to 2017, the ASRs increased for HPV-attributable cervical cancer (females; average annual percent change [AAPC] and 95% CI : 5.89% [2.82–9.06]) and penile and oropharyngeal cancers (males; 1.98% [0.82–3.15] and 9.65% [5.81–13.62], respectively). Substantial increasing risks across successive generations born between 1908 and 1978 were observed for HPV-attributable cervical cancer among females and oropharyngeal cancer in both sexes. Under a counterfactual continuation of historical trends through 2017, without incorporating subsequent vaccination or screening expansion, the ASR of HPV-attributable cervical cancer is projected to reach 19.53 (95% uncertainty interval [ UI ]: 9.99–34.36) per 100,000 in 2028–2032, compared with 10.92 (95% CI : 10.83–11.00) per 100,000 in 2013–2017. In China, the incidence of HPV-attributable cancer varies considerably according to cancer type and geography. These findings highlight the need for region-specific HPV vaccination and cancer screening strategies, particularly in areas with high incidence rates.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Current epidemiological data on cancer incidence attributable to human papillomavirus (HPV) infection in China remain unclear. This study aims to address this gap by examining the geographic and temporal variations in HPV-attributable cancer incidence.
Design and population
A population-based study was conducted in China from 2013 to 2017, with temporal trends analyzed from 1988 to 2017 and projections to 2032. Data were collected from 25 provinces, focusing on HPV-attributable cancers in both females and males.
Core methods
Joinpoint regression and age-period-cohort models were used to quantify temporal age-standardized incidence rate (ASR) trends and age and birth cohort effects. The population-attributable fraction was calculated by multiplying the high-risk HPV prevalence at each anatomical site by the specific biomarker positivity rate in HPV-positive cases.
Main results
An estimated 68,955 and 3,463 HPV-attributable cancer cases were observed among females and males in China in 2013–2017, respectively, with ASRs of 11.20 (95% confidence interval [CI]: 10.98–11.41) and 0.52 (0.20–0.82) per 100,000. From 1988 to 2017, the ASRs increased for HPV-attributable cervical cancer (females; average annual percent change [AAPC] and 95% CI: 5.89% [2.82–9.06]) and penile and oropharyngeal cancers (males; 1.98% [0.82–3.15] and 9.65% [5.81–13.62], respectively).
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the substantial increasing risks across successive generations born between 1908 and 1978 for HPV-attributable cervical cancer among females and oropharyngeal cancer in both sexes. These findings provide novel insights into the long-term trends and geographic disparities of HPV-associated cancers in China.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of region-specific HPV vaccination and cancer screening strategies, particularly in areas with high incidence rates, to address the growing burden of HPV-attributable cancers in China.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on data from the Cancer Incidence in Five Continents Volumes VII – XII, which may have limitations in data quality and completeness. The evidence is moderate, as the projections assume continuation of historical trends without accounting for future vaccination or screening interventions.

对塞拉利昂 2025 年全国 人乳头瘤病毒 多年龄段 队列研究 疫苗接种 运动的运动后评估:对选定地区的覆盖范围、意识和护理人员态度的横断面研究

Post-campaign evaluation of the 2025 national human papillomavirus multi-age cohort vaccination campaign in Sierra Leone: a cross-sectional study of coverage, awareness and caregiver attitudes in selected districts
作者： Samuel Adeyemi Williams, Desmond Maada Kangbai, Linda Samuels, Ojong-Ebot Ayukndip, Andrew K. Kemoh, Mustapha S. Kabba, Mamadu Baldeh
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估2025年塞拉利昂全国HPV多年龄队列研究 疫苗接种活动的有效性和接受度,重点关注覆盖率、知晓率以及主要照顾者对该项目的态度。
设计与对象
在 2025 年 HPV MAC 疫苗接种 运动之后,在塞拉利昂所有 5 个行政区的 10 个特意选择的地区开展了一项基于社区的横断面研究,涉及 2,253 名 11-18 岁女孩的照顾者。
核心方法
采用有目的抽样来选择地区,并使用通过移动数据收集工具管理的结构化调查问卷来收集数据。采用具有集群稳健标准误的修正泊松回归来识别与 疫苗接种 状态相关的独立因素。
主要结果
Vaccination coverage was 94.8% (2,097/2,213, 95% CI: 93.2–96.1). Composite knowledge among caregivers of vaccinated girls was 61% (1,280/2,097), with 57.3% recognising cervical cancer prevention and 46.0% knowing the HPV vaccine is a single dose.
解释与创新
研究发现,活动后态度得分、活动意识和上学率与 疫苗接种 状态独立相关,凸显了社区参与和教育在 疫苗 吸收中的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果提供了规划性学习证据,以支持塞拉利昂和其他类似地区的 HPV 从基于运动的免疫策略过渡到常规免疫策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的设计是横断面的,限制了因果推断,并且分析基于自我报告的数据,这可能会引入偏差。然而,由于样本量大和聚类稳健校正,证据是强有力的。

Post-campaign evaluation of the 2025 national human papillomavirus multi-age cohort vaccination campaign in Sierra Leone: a cross-sectional study of coverage, awareness and caregiver attitudes in selected districts

Authors: Samuel Adeyemi Williams, Desmond Maada Kangbai, Linda Samuels, Ojong-Ebot Ayukndip, Andrew K. Kemoh, Mustapha S. Kabba, Mamadu Baldeh
Original abstract (English)
The introduction of the Human Papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine for girls aged 10 years in Sierra Leone facilitated the rollout of the national Multi-Age Cohort (MAC) campaign in 2025. This study assessed HPV campaign awareness, vaccination coverage, knowledge, attitudes, barriers to vaccination, and factors associated with vaccine uptake among primary caregivers during the MAC campaign. We conducted a community-based cross-sectional study after the 2025 HPV MAC vaccination campaign in 10 purposively selected districts across all 5 administrative regions of Sierra Leone. This study included 2,253 caregivers of girls aged 11–18 years. We verified vaccination status during the interview against vaccination cards. We purposively sampled districts and collected data using a structured questionnaire administered via mobile data collection tools. We assessed knowledge only among caregivers of vaccinated girls ( n = 2,097). We used modified Poisson regression with cluster-robust standard errors as the primary analysis model to identify independent factors associated with vaccination status. Vaccination coverage was 94.8% (2,097/2,213, 95% CI: 93.2–96.1) in the evaluated districts. Composite knowledge among caregivers of vaccinated girls was 61% (1,280/2,097), 57.3% recognised cervical cancer prevention, and 46.0% knew the HPV vaccine is a single dose. Overall, attitudes toward the HPV vaccination and MAC campaign rollout were positive (mean attitude score 4.0/5.0). The post-campaign attitude score (adjusted PR: 1.060; 95% CI: 1.033–1.088, p < 0.001), campaign awareness (adjusted PR: 1.057; 95% CI: 1.013–1.102, p = 0.011), and girl attending school (adjusted PR: 1.193; 95% CI: 1.080–1.318, p < 0.001) were independently associated with vaccination status. Education level was not associated after cluster-robust correction (adjusted PR: 1.005, p = 0.477). Non-uptake was associated with access constraints, time limitations, and safety concerns. The coverage and uptake of the HPV vaccine were high in the surveyed districts. This was associated with increased awareness, post-campaign attitude score, and school attendance. The findings highlight the role of community-based health workers and nurses as central personnel in reaching girls beyond clinics and schools. These findings provide programmatic learning evidence from selected districts to inform planning the transition from campaign to routine immunisation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness and acceptance of the 2025 national HPV multi-age cohort vaccination campaign in Sierra Leone, focusing on coverage, awareness, and caregiver attitudes toward the program.
Design and population
A community-based cross-sectional study was conducted after the 2025 HPV MAC vaccination campaign in 10 purposively selected districts across all 5 administrative regions of Sierra Leone, involving 2,253 caregivers of girls aged 11–18 years.
Core methods
Purposive sampling was used to select districts, and data were collected using a structured questionnaire administered via mobile data collection tools. Modified Poisson regression with cluster-robust standard errors was employed to identify independent factors associated with vaccination status.
Main results
Vaccination coverage was 94.8% (2,097/2,213, 95% CI: 93.2–96.1). Composite knowledge among caregivers of vaccinated girls was 61% (1,280/2,097), with 57.3% recognising cervical cancer prevention and 46.0% knowing the HPV vaccine is a single dose.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that post-campaign attitude scores, campaign awareness, and school attendance were independently associated with vaccination status, highlighting the importance of community engagement and education in vaccine uptake.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide programmatic learning evidence to support the transition from campaign-based to routine immunisation strategies for HPV in Sierra Leone and other similar settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's design was cross-sectional, limiting causal inference, and the analysis was based on self-reported data, which may introduce bias. However, the evidence is strong due to the large sample size and cluster-robust correction.

苏州太湖地区女性人乳头瘤病毒感染流行病学特征、基因型分布及病毒载量的回顾性研究

Epidemiological characteristics, genotype distribution, and viral load profiles of human papillomavirus infection among women in the Taihu region of Suzhou: a retrospective study
作者： Jing Guo, Ruhong Yan, Yijie Qu
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染表现出显著的地域异质性;然而,关于中国苏州太湖地区HPV基因型分布和病毒载量特征的大规模数据仍较为有限。本研究旨在探讨该地区2023年至2025年间女性HPV感染的病毒载量、基因型谱、年龄特异性感染模式及病毒载量特征,从而为制定区域特定的预防和控制策略提供依据。本研究为一项回顾性横断面研究,纳入了2023年1月至2025年12月期间在苏州太湖地区接受HPV检测的23,196名女性。收集宫颈脱落细胞样本,并采用实时荧光定量聚合酶链反应(qPCR)检测了21种HPV基因型。在23,196名参与者中,有3,061名检测出HPV感染,总体感染率为13.20%,且三年间的感染率无显著统计学差异。年龄特异性病毒载量呈现双峰分布,≥60岁女性的感染率最高,而40–49岁女性的感染率最低。最常见的五种高危型HPV基因型为HPV52(2.70%)、HPV58(1.72%)、HPV16(1.35%)、HPV39(1.11%)和HPV56(0.96%),而低危型HPV中,HPV53(1.78%)最为常见。≥60岁女性的高危混合感染和多基因型感染比例显著高于其他年龄段。病毒载量在不同年龄段中分布不同,<30岁女性中高病毒载量最为常见(24.29%),而≥60岁女性中低病毒载量占主导(46.82%)。本研究中鉴定的基因型谱与九价疫苗所针对的基因型具有更高的理论一致性,而非二价或四价疫苗。在苏州太湖地区的女性中,检测到的主要基因型为HPV52、HPV58和HPV16。年龄较大的女性HPV阳性率、高危/低危型HPV混合感染率以及多基因型感染率更高,而低病毒载量的比例则随年龄增长而增加。主要基因型谱与九价疫苗所针对的基因型存在显著的理论重叠。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究需要解决中国苏州太湖地区 HPV 基因型分布和病毒载量特征缺乏大规模数据的问题。它旨在深入了解特定区域的 HPV 感染模式。
设计与对象
对2023年1月至2025年12月在苏州太湖地区接受HPV检测的23,196名女性进行回顾性横断面研究。总共有3,061名受试者HPV检测呈阳性,感染率为13.20%。
核心方法
采集宫颈脱落细胞样本,采用实时荧光定量聚合酶链反应(qPCR)检测21种HPV基因型。
主要结果
HPV感染的年龄特异性患病率呈双峰分布,≥60岁女性感染率最高,40~49岁女性感染率最低。≥60岁女性高危混合感染和多重感染比例明显高于其他年龄组。
解释与创新
该研究在太湖地区确定了一个独特的基因型谱,与二价或四价 疫苗 的目标基因型相比,与非价 疫苗 的目标基因型具有更高的一致性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为特定区域的 HPV 感染预防和控制策略提供信息,特别是与 ZX​​QTERMAAQXZ 选择和针对老年女性中普遍存在的高风险基因型相关的策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和数据收集中的潜在偏差而受到限制。然而,由于样本量大和全面的基因型分析,证据是强有力的。

Epidemiological characteristics, genotype distribution, and viral load profiles of human papillomavirus infection among women in the Taihu region of Suzhou: a retrospective study

Authors: Jing Guo, Ruhong Yan, Yijie Qu
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection exhibits substantial regional heterogeneity; however, large-scale data regarding genotype distribution and viral load characteristics in the Taihu region of Suzhou, China remain limited. This study aimed to investigate the prevalence, genotype spectrum, age-specific infection patterns, and viral load characteristics of HPV infection among women in this region from 2023 to 2025, thereby providing evidence for region-specific prevention and control strategies. A retrospective cross-sectional study was conducted among 23,196 women who underwent HPV testing in the Taihu region of Suzhou between January 2023 and December 2025. Cervical exfoliated cell samples were collected, and 21 HPV genotypes were detected using real-time fluorescence quantitative polymerase chain reaction (qPCR). Among the 23,196 participants, 3,061 tested positive for HPV, yielding an overall infection rate of 13.20%, with no statistically significant difference across the three study years. The age-specific prevalence demonstrated a bimodal distribution, with the highest infection rate observed in women aged ≥ 60 years and the lowest in those aged 40–49 years. The five most prevalent HR-HPV genotypes were HPV52 (2.70%), HPV58 (1.72%), HPV16 (1.35%), HPV39 (1.11%), and HPV56 (0.96%), while HPV53 (1.78%) was the most common low-risk genotype. Women aged ≥ 60 years exhibited significantly higher proportions of high-risk mixed infections and multiple infections compared with other age groups. Viral load distribution varied across age groups, with high viral loads being most common in women aged < 30 years (24.29%) and low viral loads predominating in women aged ≥ 60 years (46.82%). The genotype spectrum identified in this study showed greater concordance with the genotypes targeted by the nonavalent vaccine than with those targeted by the bivalent or quadrivalent vaccines. HPV52, HPV58, and HPV16 were the predominant genotypes detected among women in the Taihu region of Suzhou. Older women had a higher prevalence of HPV positivity, mixed HR-/LR-HPV infection, and multiple-genotype infection, whereas the proportion of low viral load measurements increased with age. The predominant genotype spectrum showed substantial theoretical overlap with the genotypes targeted by the nonavalent vaccine.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to address the lack of large-scale data on HPV genotype distribution and viral load characteristics in the Taihu region of Suzhou, China. It aimed to provide insights into region-specific patterns of HPV infection.
Design and population
A retrospective cross-sectional study was conducted among 23,196 women who underwent HPV testing in the Taihu region of Suzhou between January 2023 and December 2025. Overall, 3,061 participants tested positive for HPV, resulting in an infection rate of 13.20%.
Core methods
Cervical exfoliated cell samples were collected, and 21 HPV genotypes were detected using real-time fluorescence quantitative polymerase chain reaction (qPCR).
Main results
The age-specific prevalence of HPV infection showed a bimodal distribution, with the highest infection rate observed in women aged ≥ 60 years and the lowest in those aged 40–49 years. Women aged ≥ 60 years had significantly higher proportions of high-risk mixed infections and multiple infections compared with other age groups.
Interpretation and novelty
The study identified a distinct genotype spectrum in the Taihu region, showing greater concordance with the nonavalent vaccine's target genotypes than with those of the bivalent or quadrivalent vaccines.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform region-specific prevention and control strategies for HPV infection, particularly in relation to vaccine selection and targeting high-risk genotypes prevalent in older women.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential biases in data collection. However, the evidence is strong due to the large sample size and comprehensive genotype analysis.

在天然病毒颗粒中发现的新形式的HPV18 L1有助于病毒颗粒的稳定性和感染性

New form of HPV18 L1 found in native virions contributes to virion stability and infectivity
作者： Eric J. Ryndock, Jennifer Biryukov, Kumari Asha, Sarah Brendle, Neil D. Christensen, Craig Meyers
摘要中文翻译摘要 当前的教条指出,乳头瘤病毒病毒体仅由 L1 主要衣壳蛋白的一种形式组成。人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 已使用重组颗粒进行了研究,从而绕过了在分化上皮模型中培养病毒的需要。表达 媒介 被设计用于利用许多 HPV 类型中发现的共有蛋氨酸产生 L1。然而,HPV18 有两个额外的框内蛋氨酸残基,位于共有蛋氨酸上游 61 和 26 个氨基酸处。这些甲硫氨酸残基被排除在用于创建重组病毒的 L1 表达 媒介 之外。另一方面,由器官筏培养产生的 HPV 允许病毒粒子在其天然启动子和完整 基因组 存在的情况下在分化的上皮细胞中组装。在这里,我们利用这个系统来证明,在更自然的环境中产生的野生型 HPV18,可以从上游甲硫氨酸(共有甲硫氨酸上游的 61 个氨基酸残基)翻译出较大形式的 L1,以及从共有甲硫氨酸中翻译出较小的形式,两种尺寸都在新形成的病毒体中组装。61位上游甲硫氨酸残基的消融改变了病毒粒子衣壳构象,并且还降低了病毒粒子稳定性和感染性。重要性 本研究调查乳头瘤病毒病毒颗粒是否含有不止一种 L1 主要衣壳蛋白亚型。通过使用模仿自然分化上皮的器官型筏培养物,我们发现野生型 HPV18 表达两种不同的 L1 亚型并将其整合到病毒粒子中,包括从上游甲硫氨酸翻译而来的以前未被识别的较长形式。我们的研究进一步表明,上游蛋氨酸的破坏会损害病毒粒子的稳定性和感染性,证明较长的 L1 亚型有助于适当的衣壳结构和病毒功能。这些发现提供了对 HPV 衣壳生物学的新认识,并对 HPV 疫苗、诊断和 抗病毒药物 疗法的未来发展具有重要意义。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查乳头瘤病毒病毒粒子是否含有不止一种 L1 主要衣壳蛋白亚型,挑战了当前病毒粒子仅由一种形式的 L1 组成的教条。
设计与对象
该研究利用器官型筏培养物来模拟自然分化的上皮,从而产生具有天然启动子和完整 基因组 的野生型 HPV18 病毒粒子。
核心方法
研究人员采用器官型筏培养物产生 HPV18 病毒颗粒,并分析了两种不同 L1 亚型的表达和掺入,包括从上游甲硫氨酸翻译而来的较长形式。
主要结果
61位上游甲硫氨酸残基的消融改变了病毒体衣壳构象,降低了病毒体稳定性和感染性,并揭示了野生型HPV18表达两种不同的L1亚型并将其整合到病毒体中。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,较长的 L1 亚型有助于正确的衣壳结构和病毒功能,为 HPV 衣壳生物学提供了新的认识。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现对 HPV 疫苗、诊断和 抗病毒药物 疗法的未来发展具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,并且结果基于单一 HPV 类型,这可能会影响普遍性。

New form of HPV18 L1 found in native virions contributes to virion stability and infectivity

Authors: Eric J. Ryndock, Jennifer Biryukov, Kumari Asha, Sarah Brendle, Neil D. Christensen, Craig Meyers
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Current dogma states that papillomavirus virions consist of only one form of the L1 major capsid protein. Human papillomaviruses (HPVs) have been studied using recombinant particles, thereby bypassing the need to grow the virus in a model of differentiating epithelium. Expression vectors engineered to produce L1 utilize a consensus methionine found in many HPV types. However, HPV18 has two additional in-frame methionine residues located 61 and 26 amino acids upstream of the consensus methionine. These methionine residues are excluded from the L1 expression vector used to create the recombinant virus. On the other hand, HPV produced from organotypic raft culture allows virions to be assembled in a differentiating epithelium in the presence of its native promoters and complete genome. Here, we have utilized this system to show that the wild-type HPV18, produced in a more natural setting, translates a larger form of L1 from the upstream methionine, which is 61 amino acid residues upstream from the consensus methionine, and a smaller form from the consensus methionine, with both sizes assembled in the newly formed virion. Ablation of the upstream methionine residue at position 61 altered the virion capsid conformation, and also decreased virion stability and infectivity. IMPORTANCE The present study investigates whether the papillomavirus virions contain more than one isoform of the L1 major capsid protein. By using organotypic raft cultures that mimic the naturally differentiating epithelium, we revealed that wild-type HPV18 expresses and incorporates two distinct L1 isoforms into the virion, including a previously unrecognized longer form translated from an upstream methionine. Our study has further shown that disruption of the upstream methionine impairs virion stability and infectivity, demonstrating that the longer L1 isoform contributes to proper capsid architecture and viral function. These findings offer a new understanding of HPV capsid biology and have significant implications for the future development of HPV vaccines, diagnostics, and antiviral therapies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether papillomavirus virions contain more than one isoform of the L1 major capsid protein, challenging the current dogma that virions consist of only one form of L1.
Design and population
The study utilized organotypic raft cultures to mimic the naturally differentiating epithelium, allowing the production of wild-type HPV18 virions with native promoters and complete genome.
Core methods
The researchers employed organotypic raft cultures to produce HPV18 virions and analyzed the expression and incorporation of two distinct L1 isoforms, including a longer form translated from an upstream methionine.
Main results
Ablation of the upstream methionine residue at position 61 altered the virion capsid conformation, decreased virion stability and infectivity, and revealed that wild-type HPV18 expresses and incorporates two distinct L1 isoforms into the virion.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that the longer L1 isoform contributes to proper capsid architecture and viral function, offering a new understanding of HPV capsid biology.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings have significant implications for the future development of HPV vaccines, diagnostics, and antiviral therapies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the results are based on a single HPV type, which may affect generalizability.

印度南部某三级护理教学机构本科医学生关于 人乳头瘤病毒 感染和 HPV 疫苗接种 的知识、态度和实践:一项横断面研究

Knowledge, Attitude and Practice regarding Human Papillomavirus Infection and HPV Vaccination among Undergraduate Medical Students at a Tertiary Care Teaching Institution in South India: A Cross-sectional Study
作者： Monisha Subbiah, Shriram Natarajan, Renji Sakunthala Raveendran
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解印度南部医学本科生对 HPV 感染和 HPV 疫苗接种 的知识、态度和实践,以解决印度国家免疫运动背景下的知识差距。
设计与对象
对印度泰米尔纳德邦一所三级护理教学机构的 MBBS 本科生进行了一项横断面研究,重点关注女学生作为主要目标人群。
核心方法
使用预先测试的自填问卷来评估参与者的社会人口统计项目、知识、态度和实践/障碍项目。
主要结果
285名参与者中,女性占53.3%,知识良好的占60.4%,态度积极的占63.2%。疫苗 覆盖率较低,为 11.8%,134 名未接种疫苗的学生中有 76 人愿意在教育课程结束后接种疫苗。
解释与创新
良好的知识和积极的态度与之前对 疫苗 的认识相关,但自我报告的 HPV 疫苗接种 摄取率仍然较低,凸显了知识与实践之间的差距。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,结构化教育和基于校园的 疫苗接种 可以提高女医学生中 HPV 疫苗接种 的覆盖率,支持印度的公共卫生工作。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横断面设计和对自我报告数据的依赖限制了研究结果的普遍性,并且样本是从泰米尔纳德邦的一个机构中抽取的。

Knowledge, Attitude and Practice regarding Human Papillomavirus Infection and HPV Vaccination among Undergraduate Medical Students at a Tertiary Care Teaching Institution in South India: A Cross-sectional Study

Authors: Monisha Subbiah, Shriram Natarajan, Renji Sakunthala Raveendran
Original abstract (English)
Background: Cervical cancer is an HPV (human papillomavirus) associated disease with India contributing to a disproportionate share of the worldwide burden. Undergraduate female medical students are an at-risk group and future supporters of vaccination yet the gap between knowledge and personal vaccine uptake remains inadequately characterized, especially since India added HPV vaccine to its national immunization campaign. Methods: A questionnaire based cross-sectional study was conducted among all MBBS undergraduates studying in a tertiary care teaching institution in Tamil Nadu using a pre-tested self-administered instrument consisting of sociodemographic items, 15 items of knowledge scale, six items of Likert attitude scale and practice/barrier items. Vaccine coverage was analyzed in female students, the main target population for prevention of cervical cancer. Results: Among the 285 participants, 53.3% were female, and the mean age was 20.3 ± 1.6 years. Good knowledge was present in 172 (60.4%; mean score 10.59 ± 3.78) and a positive attitude in 175 of 277 (63.2%; Cronbach’s α = 0.875). The poorest-answered items concerned the dosing schedule (34.4% correct) and the national-programme status of the vaccine (57.2%). Among female students, self-reported HPV vaccination uptake was 11.8% (18/152), yet 76 of 134 unvaccinated female students (56.7%) were willing to be vaccinated and 80 (59.7%) would accept vaccination after an educational session; the most frequently reported barrier was intention to be vaccinated later (33.3%), followed by lack of information (21.8%). Good knowledge was independently associated with prior awareness of the vaccine (aOR 2.66, 95% CI 1.40–5.04); a positive attitude was less likely in male students (aOR 0.56, 95% CI 0.33–0.94) and more likely with prior vaccine awareness (aOR 1.98, 95% CI 1.04–3.75). Conclusion: Despite moderate-to-good knowledge and favourable attitudes, self-reported HPV vaccination uptake remained low among female MBBS students, highlighting a clear knowledge–practice gap. High willingness to learn and vaccinate suggests that structured education and campus-based vaccination could improve coverage.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the knowledge, attitude, and practice regarding HPV infection and HPV vaccination among undergraduate medical students in South India, addressing a knowledge gap in the context of India's national immunization campaign.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among MBBS undergraduates in a tertiary care teaching institution in Tamil Nadu, India, focusing on female students as the main target population.
Core methods
A pre-tested self-administered questionnaire was used to assess sociodemographic items, knowledge, attitude, and practice/barrier items among the participants.
Main results
Among 285 participants, 53.3% were female, with good knowledge in 60.4% and a positive attitude in 63.2%. Vaccine coverage was low at 11.8%, with 76 of 134 unvaccinated students willing to be vaccinated after an educational session.
Interpretation and novelty
Good knowledge and positive attitudes were associated with prior awareness of the vaccine, but self-reported HPV vaccination uptake remained low, highlighting a knowledge-practice gap.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that structured education and campus-based vaccination could improve HPV vaccination coverage among female medical students, supporting public health efforts in India.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's cross-sectional design and reliance on self-reported data limit the generalizability of the findings, and the sample was drawn from a single institution in Tamil Nadu.

中国山西省女性患病率及人乳头瘤病毒感染和宫颈细胞学病变的危险因素:一项横断面研究

Prevalence and risk factors of human papillomavirus infection and cervical cytological lesions among women in Shanxi, China: a cross-sectional study
作者： Shanshan Ge, Haining Gao, Jing Wang, Siyao Guo, Yunqi Chang, Yueyue Qi, Jingrui Han, You Jia, Jianyu Dong, Feixue Wei
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估中国山西省女性中人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染和宫颈细胞学病变的患病率及风险因素,以提供监测疫苗影响和改进宫颈癌预防策略的基线数据。
设计与对象
本研究在中国山西省开展,研究对象为2021年1月至2024年12月期间接受常规健康检查并同时进行HPV DNA检测和宫颈细胞学检查的19,384名女性。
核心方法
通过基因型特异性及分组分析估计HPV 患病率,并采用逻辑回归分析评估高危型(HR-)HPV感染和细胞学异常相关的因素。
主要结果
High-risk HPV prevalence was 10.9%, with HPV52, HPV16, and HPV58 being the most frequent genotypes. HR-HPV prevalence was higher in women aged ≥ 56 years (AOR = 2.0, 95% CI 1.6–2.3) and lower in married women (AOR = 0.5, 95% CI 0.4–0.7).
解释与创新
本研究发现,HPV基因型分布因细胞学异常的不同等级而显著变化,其中HPV16/18在高度宫颈异常中占较大比例。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为监测HPV 疫苗接种的影响提供了关键的基线数据,并有助于基于基因型特异性风险评估的针对性宫颈癌预防策略的制定。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系,并且可能存在混杂因素。由于该研究的观察性质,证据是中等的。

Prevalence and risk factors of human papillomavirus infection and cervical cytological lesions among women in Shanxi, China: a cross-sectional study

Authors: Shanshan Ge, Haining Gao, Jing Wang, Siyao Guo, Yunqi Chang, Yueyue Qi, Jingrui Han, You Jia, Jianyu Dong, Feixue Wei
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background With the introduction of human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination into China’s national immunization programme in November 2025, robust baseline evidence on HPV epidemiology is critical. Data on HPV prevalence, genotype-specific distribution, risk factors, and the association between HPV infection and cervical cytology will provide a reference for monitoring vaccine impact and optimizing future cervical cancer prevention strategies. Methods We conducted a retrospective cross-sectional study of women undergoing routine health examinations with concurrent HPV DNA testing and cervical cytology between January 2021 to December 2024 in Shanxi, China. We estimated genotype-specific and grouped HPV prevalence, assessed single and multiple infections, and compared genotype distributions across cytological categories. Logistic regression was used to assess factors associated with high-risk (HR-) HPV infection and cytological abnormalities. Results Among 19,384 women (median age 42 years, interquartile range 36–51), HR-HPV prevalence was 10.9%. The most frequent HR-HPV genotypes were HPV52 (2.3%), HPV16 (1.7%), and HPV58 (1.7%). The prevalence of HPV16/18 and HPV6/11/16/18/31/33/45/52/58 was 2.3% and 7.4%, respectively. HR-HPV prevalence was higher in women aged ≥ 56 years (adjusted odds ratio [AOR] = 2.0, 95% confidence interval [CI] 1.6–2.3 for women aged 56–65 years compared to those aged 20–35 years) and lower in married women (AOR = 0.5, 95% CI 0.4–0.7). Abnormal cytology was observed in 2.1% of participants. Among HPV-positive women, HPV16/18 prevalence increased from low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (LSIL) to atypical squamous cells cannot exclude HSIL (ASC-H)/high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (HSIL) (OR = 3.4, 95% CI 1.0–11.5), while HPV31/33/45/52/58 prevalence showed a moderate but non-significant increase (1.8, 95% CI 0.6–5.6). Non-16/18/31/33/45/52/58 HR-HPV genotypes decreased with severity (OR = 0.6, 95% CI 0.1–2.2). Conclusions HR-HPV infection was common among women undergoing health examinations in Shanxi with HPV52, HPV16, and HPV58 being the predominant genotypes, and HR-HPV prevalence increased with age. HPV genotype distributions varied substantially across different grades of cytological abnormalities, with HPV16/18 accounting for a larger proportion of high-grade cervical abnormalities. These findings highlight the importance of genotype-specific risk assessment in supporting genotype-specific risk assessment in HPV vaccination and cervical cancer prevention strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the prevalence and risk factors of human papillomavirus (HPV) infection and cervical cytological lesions in Shanxi, China, to provide baseline data for monitoring vaccine impact and improving cervical cancer prevention strategies.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among 19,384 women undergoing routine health examinations in Shanxi, China, between January 2021 and December 2024, with concurrent HPV DNA testing and cervical cytology.
Core methods
HPV prevalence was estimated using genotype-specific and grouped analysis, and logistic regression was employed to evaluate factors associated with high-risk (HR-) HPV infection and cytological abnormalities.
Main results
High-risk HPV prevalence was 10.9%, with HPV52, HPV16, and HPV58 being the most frequent genotypes. HR-HPV prevalence was higher in women aged ≥ 56 years (AOR = 2.0, 95% CI 1.6–2.3) and lower in married women (AOR = 0.5, 95% CI 0.4–0.7).
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that HPV genotype distributions varied significantly across different grades of cytological abnormalities, with HPV16/18 accounting for a larger proportion of high-grade cervical abnormalities.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide critical baseline data for monitoring the impact of HPV vaccination and inform targeted cervical cancer prevention strategies based on genotype-specific risk assessments.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality, and the potential for confounding factors. The evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the study.

HPV16 和 HPV18 E7 免疫组织化学在宫颈病变进展中的诊断效用和组织定位:与 PCR 的比较研究

Diagnostic utility and tissue localization of HPV16 and HPV18 E7 immunohistochemistry in cervical lesion progression: a comparative study with PCR
作者： Fatimah Suliman Mohammad Aljebaly
摘要中文翻译背景 高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV),特别是 HPV16 和 HPV18 的持续感染,通过病毒 E7 癌蛋白的持续表达导致宫颈癌发生。尽管 HPV DNA 聚合酶链反应 (PCR) 广泛用于病毒检测,但它并不能直接反映组织内具有生物活性的致癌转化。本研究评估了 HPV16 和 HPV18 E7 免疫组织化学 (IHC) 在宫颈病变进展过程中的诊断效用、临床病理学意义、组织定位和一致性。方法对109例代表非肿瘤、癌前和恶性病变的福尔马林固定石蜡包埋宫颈组织标本进行回顾性研究。通过免疫组织化学评估 HPV16 和 HPV18 E7 蛋白表达,并与 HPV DNA PCR 结果进行比较。进行一致性分析、受试者工作特征(ROC)分析和多变量逻辑回归来评估诊断性能和独立预测价值。结果 HPV16和HPV18 E7表达随病变严重程度逐渐增加,在高级别鳞状上皮内病变和浸润性癌中观察到免疫反应性最高。两种 HPV 类型的 IHC 和 PCR 结果之间观察到显着的一致性。与 HPV18(90.0%、79.3%、83.7% 和 0.894)相比,HPV16 表现出卓越的诊断性能,敏感性、特异性、准确性和曲线下面积 (AUC) 值分别为 92.3%、83.9%、89.9% 和 0.925。多变量逻辑回归将 HPV16 和 HPV18 E7 表达确定为疾病状态的重要独立预测因子。组织病理学评估显示渐进的定位模式,从低级别病变中的局灶性基底/副基底染色转变为高级别病变和浸润性癌中的弥漫性全层上皮和肿瘤相关表达。结论 HPV16 和 HPV18 E7 免疫组织化学显示与宫颈病变严重程度密切相关,并且与 PCR 结果基本一致,支持其作为宫颈病理学中基于组织的补充生物标志物的潜在用途。研究结果进一步表明,E7 免疫组织化学可以提供有关生物活性 HPV 驱动的转化的临床相关信息,并改善诊断困难病例中的病变特征。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 HPV16 和 HPV18 E7 免疫组织化学在宫颈病变进展中的诊断效用和组织定位,解决 PCR 在反映组织内生物活性致癌转化方面的局限性。
设计与对象
对 109 份代表非肿瘤、癌前和恶性病变的福尔马林固定石蜡包埋宫颈组织标本进行了回顾性研究。
核心方法
通过免疫组织化学评估 HPV16 和 HPV18 E7 蛋白表达,并与 HPV DNA PCR 结果进行比较。进行一致性分析、受试者工作特征(ROC)分析和多变量逻辑回归来评估诊断性能和独立预测价值。
主要结果
HPV16和HPV18的E7表达随着病变程度的加重而逐渐增加,在高级别鳞状上皮内病变和浸润性癌中观察到最高的免疫反应性。HPV16在敏感性、特异性、准确性和曲线下面积(AUC)方面均表现出优于HPV18的诊断性能,分别为92.3%、83.9%、89.9%和0.925,而HPV18分别为90.0%、79.3%、83.7%和0.894。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,HPV16和HPV18的E7免疫组化与宫颈病变程度有很强的关联性,并且与PCR结果有显著的一致性,支持其作为宫颈病理学中互补的组织生物标志物的潜在应用价值。
科研与公卫意义
结果进一步表明,E7免疫组化可能提供与生物活性HPV驱动转化相关的临床相关信息,并有助于在诊断困难的病例中改善病变特征的判断。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到回顾性设计和小样本量的限制,这可能会影响其结果的普遍适用性。然而,免疫组化与PCR结果之间高度的一致性以及HPV16优越的诊断性能支持了这些结果的可靠性。

Diagnostic utility and tissue localization of HPV16 and HPV18 E7 immunohistochemistry in cervical lesion progression: a comparative study with PCR

Authors: Fatimah Suliman Mohammad Aljebaly
Original abstract (English)
Background Persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV), particularly HPV16 and HPV18, contributes to cervical carcinogenesis through sustained expression of the viral E7 oncoprotein. Although HPV DNA polymerase chain reaction (PCR) is widely used for viral detection, it does not directly reflect biologically active oncogenic transformation within tissue. This study evaluated the diagnostic utility, clinicopathological significance, tissue localization, and concordance of HPV16 and HPV18 E7 immunohistochemistry (IHC) across the spectrum of cervical lesion progression. Methods A retrospective study was conducted on 109 formalin-fixed paraffin-embedded cervical tissue specimens representing non-neoplastic, premalignant, and malignant lesions. HPV16 and HPV18 E7 protein expression was assessed by immunohistochemistry and compared with HPV DNA PCR findings. Concordance analysis, receiver operating characteristic (ROC) analysis, and multivariable logistic regression were performed to evaluate diagnostic performance and independent predictive value. Results HPV16 and HPV18 E7 expression increased progressively with lesion severity, with the highest immunoreactivity observed in high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions and invasive carcinoma. Substantial concordance was observed between IHC and PCR findings for both HPV types. HPV16 demonstrated superior diagnostic performance, with sensitivity, specificity, accuracy, and area under the curve (AUC) values of 92.3%, 83.9%, 89.9%, and 0.925, respectively, compared with HPV18 (90.0%, 79.3%, 83.7%, and 0.894). Multivariable logistic regression identified HPV16 and HPV18 E7 expression as significant independent predictors of disease status. Histopathological evaluation demonstrated progressive localization patterns, transitioning from focal basal/parabasal staining in low-grade lesions to diffuse full-thickness epithelial and tumor-associated expression in high-grade lesions and invasive carcinoma. Conclusions HPV16 and HPV18 E7 immunohistochemistry demonstrated strong association with cervical lesion severity and substantial concordance with PCR findings, supporting its potential utility as a complementary tissue-based biomarker in cervical pathology. The findings further suggest that E7 immunohistochemistry may provide clinically relevant information regarding biologically active HPV-driven transformation and improve lesion characterization in diagnostically challenging cases.
View research seven-element analysis

The study aimed to evaluate the diagnostic utility and tissue localization of HPV16 and HPV18 E7 immunohistochemistry in cervical lesion progression, addressing the limitations of PCR in reflecting biologically active oncogenic transformation within tissue.
Design and population

HPV16 demonstrated superior diagnostic performance with sensitivity, specificity, accuracy, and area under the curve (AUC) values of 92.3%, 83.9%, 89.9%, and 0.925, respectively, compared with HPV18 (90.0%, 79.3%, 83.7%, and 0.894).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that HPV16 and HPV18 E7 immunohistochemistry provide strong association with cervical lesion severity and substantial concordance with PCR findings, supporting its potential utility as a complementary tissue-based biomarker in cervical pathology.

Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and the use of a small sample size, which may affect generalizability. However, the high concordance between IHC and PCR findings and the superior diagnostic performance of HPV16 support the reliability of the results.

18-25岁女性中多重高危型HPV感染及机会性HPV感染:2024年横断面研究

Multiple High-Risk HPV Infections and Opportunistic HPV Prevalence Among Women Aged 18–25 Years with NILM, ASC-US, or LSIL Cytology: A 2024 Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Erik Kúdela, Tomáš Rokos, Erik Kozubík, Terézia Pribulová, Filip Piovar, Kamil Biringer, Erik Kudela, Erik Kozubik, Terezia Pribulova
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在年轻女性中,多重高危型HPV(hrHPV)感染在年龄和细胞学检查方面特征不明确,尤其是在部分基因分型的情况下。
设计与对象
这项回顾性横断面研究分析了2024年来自斯洛伐克北部的实验室数据。在7613名唯一女性中,7574名符合细胞学检查标准;804名接受了cobas 4800检测,其中295名为hrHPV阳性。
核心方法
该研究使用 2024 年的实验室数据并应用 cobas 4800 检测来识别 hrHPV 阳性。多重感染定义为同时检测到至少两种可报告类别(HPV16、HPV18 或其他 hrHPV)。
主要结果
Multiple infection, defined as concurrent detection of at least two reportable categories (HPV16, HPV18, or other hrHPV), occurred in 69/295 women (23.4%; 95% CI, 18.9–28.5%). Among women with multiple infections, HPV16 combined with other hrHPV was the most frequent pattern (30/69; 43.5%).
解释与创新
在年龄调整后,细胞学检查未观察到统计学上显著的差异(p = 0.471);在细胞学调整后,年龄也未观察到统计学上显著的差异(p = 0.891)。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,年轻女性中多重hrHPV感染较为常见,但其并不显著随细胞学检查或年龄变化,这可能为未来的监测和预防策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
NILM的估计值描述了一种自我选择的预防性检测,并不代表整个人群。由于研究设计为回顾性,且可能存在选择偏倚,证据强度中等。

Multiple High-Risk HPV Infections and Opportunistic HPV Prevalence Among Women Aged 18–25 Years with NILM, ASC-US, or LSIL Cytology: A 2024 Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Erik Kúdela, Tomáš Rokos, Erik Kozubík, Terézia Pribulová, Filip Piovar, Kamil Biringer, Erik Kudela, Erik Kozubik, Terezia Pribulova
Original abstract (English)
Multiple high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) infections in young women are poorly characterized across age and cytology, particularly with partial genotyping. We assessed multiple hrHPV infections among HPV-positive women aged 18–25 years with NILM, ASC-US, or LSIL; the secondary objective was hrHPV positivity among electively tested women with NILM. This retrospective cross-sectional study analyzed 2024 laboratory data from northern Slovakia. Of 7613 unique women, 7574 met cytological eligibility criteria; 804 underwent cobas 4800 testing, and 295 were hrHPV-positive. Multiple infection, defined as concurrent detection of at least two reportable categories (HPV16, HPV18, or other hrHPV), occurred in 69/295 women (23.4%; 95% CI, 18.9–28.5%). No statistically detectable variation was observed by cytology after age adjustment (p = 0.471) or by age after cytology adjustment (p = 0.891). Among women with multiple infections, HPV16 combined with other hrHPV was the most frequent pattern (30/69; 43.5%). Among 396 electively tested women with NILM, 75 were hrHPV-positive (18.9%; 95% CI, 15.4–23.1%); positivity was lower at ages 23–25 than 18–22 (10.6% vs. 26.6%; OR, 0.33; 95% CI, 0.19–0.57; p < 0.001). The NILM estimate describes a self-selected preventive cohort and is not population-representative.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Multiple high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) infections in young women are poorly characterized across age and cytology, particularly with partial genotyping.
Design and population
This retrospective cross-sectional study analyzed 2024 laboratory data from northern Slovakia. Of 7613 unique women, 7574 met cytological eligibility criteria; 804 underwent cobas 4800 testing, and 295 were hrHPV-positive.
Core methods
The study used laboratory data from 2024 and applied cobas 4800 testing to identify hrHPV positivity. Multiple infection was defined as concurrent detection of at least two reportable categories (HPV16, HPV18, or other hrHPV).
Main results
Multiple infection, defined as concurrent detection of at least two reportable categories (HPV16, HPV18, or other hrHPV), occurred in 69/295 women (23.4%; 95% CI, 18.9–28.5%). Among women with multiple infections, HPV16 combined with other hrHPV was the most frequent pattern (30/69; 43.5%).
Interpretation and novelty
No statistically detectable variation was observed by cytology after age adjustment (p = 0.471) or by age after cytology adjustment (p = 0.891).
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that multiple hrHPV infections are common in young women, but they do not vary significantly by cytology or age, which may inform future surveillance and prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The NILM estimate describes a self-selected preventive cohort and is not population-representative. The evidence is moderate due to the retrospective design and potential selection bias.

筛选时温度、颗粒物和绿度与 人乳头瘤病毒 阳性的关联:使用树状分布滞后非线性模型对广东省(2016-2022 年)的分析

Associations of temperature, particulate matter, and greenness with human papillomavirus positivity at screening: an analysis of Guangdong Province (2016–2022) using the treed distributed lag nonlinear model
作者： Xiaoshan Hong, Zu-Ming You, Li Wu, Qiaowen Bu, Chenlu Yang, Lulu He, Zhijiang Liang, Xiping Luo, Guangming Tan
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查中国广东省(2016-2022 年)筛查时温度、颗粒物和绿色与 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 阳性的关联,解决影响 HPV 阳性的环境因素的有限证据。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 2016 年至 2022 年中国广东省 1,287,412 名女性的健康检查数据,使用树分布滞后非线性模型来评估与 HPV 阳性的滞后关联。
核心方法
收集了 10 个协变量,并按检查日期分配温度、颗粒物 2.5 (PM2.5) 和归一化植被指数 (NDVI)。应用树分布滞后非线性模型来评估筛查时与 HPV 阳性的滞后关联。
主要结果
Low temperature was associated with higher cumulative odds of HPV positivity at shorter lags (cOR = 1.303, 95% CrI, 1.194, 1.428), whereas high temperature showed a longer-lag association at longer lags (cOR = 1.063, 95% CrI, 1.007, 1.122). High PM2.5 and low NDVI were associated with higher odds of HPV positivity over various lag weeks.
解释与创新
该研究确定了 HPV 阳性的预筛查环境相关性,其中与较短滞后的低温和较长滞后的高温相关性更强。在主要 24 周分析中,高 PM2.5 和低 NDVI 也与较高的几率相关。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,环境因素,特别是温度和空气污染,可能会影响 HPV 阳性,并需要通过重复 HPV 检测和更全面地控制时间、空间、行为和社会经济因素的纵向研究来证实。
局限与证据强度
该研究的设计局限性包括横截面数据的使用和潜在的混杂因素。由于研究的观察性质和纵向确认的需要,证据强度中等。

Associations of temperature, particulate matter, and greenness with human papillomavirus positivity at screening: an analysis of Guangdong Province (2016–2022) using the treed distributed lag nonlinear model

Authors: Xiaoshan Hong, Zu-Ming You, Li Wu, Qiaowen Bu, Chenlu Yang, Lulu He, Zhijiang Liang, Xiping Luo, Guangming Tan
Original abstract (English)
Introduction Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a double-stranded DNA virus that may cause cervical, vulvar, and anal cancers in women. Environmental factors including temperature, air pollution, and greenness may be associated with HPV positivity; however, evidence remains limited. Methods This study analysed health examination data of 1,287,412 women in Guangdong Province, China (2016-2022). Ten covariates were collected, and temperature, particulate matter 2.5 (PM2.5), and normalized difference vegetation index (NDVI) were assigned by examination date. Tree distributed lag non-linear models were applied to evaluate lagged associations with HPV positivity at screening. Results Using the Q50 of biweekly mean temperature, PM2.5, and NDVI (25.594°C, 20.300 μg/m3, and 0.544, respectively) as references, low temperature (Q2.5, 13.300°C) was associated with higher cumulative odds of HPV positivity at Lag Weeks 3-8 (cOR = 1.303, 95% CrI, 1.194, 1.428), whereas high temperature (Q97.5, 29.851°C) showed a longer-lag association at Lag Weeks 9-14 (cOR = 1.063, 95% CrI, 1.007, 1.122). High PM2.5 (Q97.5, 38.021μg/m3) and low NDVI (Q2.5, 0.229) were associated with higher odds of HPV positivity over Lag Weeks 1-16 (cOR = 1.172, 95% CrI, 1.051, 1.283) and Lag Weeks 5-18 (cOR = 1.318, 95% CrI, 1.131, 1.566), respectively. Stratified analyses suggested stronger low-temperature associations among middle-aged and older women and among women in the Pearl River Delta and Western Guangdong. Discussion In conclusion, temperature showed lag-dependent associations with screening-detected HPV positivity, with stronger associations for low temperature at shorter lags and high temperature at longer lags. High PM2.5 and low NDVI were also associated with higher odds in the primary 24-week analyses. Temporal sensitivity analyses attenuated the PM2.5 association and weakened the NDVI association under the 36-week specification, whereas the low-temperature association was comparatively stable and uncertainty increased for high temperature. These findings identify pre-screening environmental correlates of HPV positivity and warrant confirmation in longitudinal studies with repeated HPV testing and more comprehensive control of temporal, spatial, behavioral, and socioeconomic factors.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the associations of temperature, particulate matter, and greenness with human papillomavirus (HPV) positivity at screening in Guangdong Province, China (2016–2022), addressing the limited evidence on environmental factors influencing HPV positivity.
Design and population
The study analysed health examination data of 1,287,412 women in Guangdong Province, China, from 2016 to 2022, using tree distributed lag non-linear models to evaluate lagged associations with HPV positivity.
Core methods
Ten covariates were collected, and temperature, particulate matter 2.5 (PM2.5), and normalized difference vegetation index (NDVI) were assigned by examination date. Tree distributed lag non-linear models were applied to evaluate lagged associations with HPV positivity at screening.
Main results
Low temperature was associated with higher cumulative odds of HPV positivity at shorter lags (cOR = 1.303, 95% CrI, 1.194, 1.428), whereas high temperature showed a longer-lag association at longer lags (cOR = 1.063, 95% CrI, 1.007, 1.122). High PM2.5 and low NDVI were associated with higher odds of HPV positivity over various lag weeks.
Interpretation and novelty
The study identified pre-screening environmental correlates of HPV positivity, with stronger associations for low temperature at shorter lags and high temperature at longer lags. High PM2.5 and low NDVI were also associated with higher odds in the primary 24-week analyses.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that environmental factors, particularly temperature and air pollution, may influence HPV positivity and warrant confirmation in longitudinal studies with repeated HPV testing and more comprehensive control of temporal, spatial, behavioral, and socioeconomic factors.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's design limitations include the use of cross-sectional data and potential confounding factors. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and the need for longitudinal confirmation.

绝经状态对塞内加尔中年妇女 人乳头瘤病毒 检测和宫颈肿瘤的影响

Impact of menopausal status on human papillomavirus detection and cervical neoplasia in middle-aged Senegalese women
作者： Emily R. Spencer, Rachel L. Winer, Selly Ba, Marie Pierre Sy, John Lin, Qinghua Feng, Geoffrey S. Gottlieb, Papa Salif Sow, Nancy B. Kiviat, Stephen E. Hawes
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究的目的是评估 40-60 岁塞内加尔女性的绝经状态是否与 HPV 和宫颈肿瘤检测相关。
设计与对象
我们对塞内加尔达喀尔(1998-2011)的三项前瞻性 宫颈癌 研究进行了二次分析。
核心方法
使用多变量逻辑回归和广义估计方程来估计调整后的比值比和 95% 置信区间,并根据年龄、妊娠和 HIV 状况进行调整。
主要结果
Odds of cervical neoplasia were elevated, but non-significant, among postmenopausal women (OR = 1.36; 95% CI: 0.97, 1.90), with increased odds for both high-grade (OR = 1.43; 95% CI: 0.90, 2.28) and low-grade lesions (OR = 1.30; 95% CI: 0.84, 2.02).
解释与创新
这些发现表明,更年期可能导致 HPV 持续存在或重新激活,以及宫颈肿瘤的发展。
科研与公卫意义
我们的结果强调了持续 监测 对老年女性 HPV 和宫颈肿瘤的重要性,特别是在 宫颈癌 负担过高的资源匮乏环境中。
局限与证据强度
然而,在仅限于已知艾滋病毒状况的参与者的敏感性分析中,这些宫颈肿瘤发生的几率被减弱,这表明在解释这些发现的严重程度时要谨慎。

Impact of menopausal status on human papillomavirus detection and cervical neoplasia in middle-aged Senegalese women

Authors: Emily R. Spencer, Rachel L. Winer, Selly Ba, Marie Pierre Sy, John Lin, Qinghua Feng, Geoffrey S. Gottlieb, Papa Salif Sow, Nancy B. Kiviat, Stephen E. Hawes
Original abstract (English)
Hormonal and immunologic changes related to menopause may play a role in human papillomavirus (HPV) and cervical neoplasia detection, yet this relationship has not been examined in West African populations, where cervical cancer remains a leading cause of cancer-related death. The purpose of this study was to assess whether menopausal status is associated with HPV and cervical neoplasia detection in Senegalese women aged 40–60. We conducted a secondary analysis of three prospective cervical cancer studies in Dakar, Senegal (1998–2011). Participants with known menopausal status and a satisfactory HPV test (N = 3,118) and/or Pap smear (N = 2,907) were included, excluding those with cervical cancer or pregnancy; participants without known HIV status were additionally excluded from the cervical neoplasia analysis. Multivariable logistic regression and generalized estimating equations were used to estimate adjusted odds ratios and 95% confidence intervals, adjusting for age, gravidity, and HIV status. Compared with premenopausal women, postmenopausal women had significantly higher odds of HPV detection (OR = 1.48; 95% CI: 1.18, 1.85), including both high-risk (OR = 1.55; 95% CI: 1.25, 1.93) and low-risk types (OR = 1.64; 95% CI: 1.30, 2.08). Odds of cervical neoplasia were elevated, but non-significant, among postmenopausal women (OR = 1.36; 95% CI: 0.97, 1.90), with increased odds for both high-grade (OR = 1.43; 95% CI: 0.90, 2.28) and low-grade lesions (OR = 1.30; 95% CI: 0.84, 2.02). However, in a sensitivity analysis restricted to participants with known HIV status, these cervical neoplasia odds were attenuated, suggesting caution in interpreting the magnitude of these findings. These findings suggest menopause may contribute to HPV persistence or reactivation, and development of cervical neoplasia. Our results highlight the importance of continued surveillance for HPV and cervical neoplasia among older women, particularly in low-resource settings with disproportionately high cervical cancer burden.

Association of Receipt of Human Papillomavirus Vaccination and Personal History of Cancer Diagnosis in the United States: A Cross-Sectional Study Using NHIS Data

Authors: Manali Desai, Shreela V. Sharma, Joël Fokom Domgue, Robert Yu, Wenyaw Chan, Charles Darkoh, Sanjay Shete
Original abstract (English)
Background: Cancer survivors are at increased risk for developing subsequent cancers, including HPV-associated cancers, compared to the general population. However, evidence regarding the receipt of HPV vaccination and cancer diagnosis status is limited. Methods: We analyzed 2019 and 2022 National Health Interview Survey data, focusing on adults aged 18–45 years who were eligible for HPV vaccination. We performed descriptive statistics and evaluated associations using survey-weighted bivariable and multivariable logistic regression to assess the association between a personal history of any cancer diagnosis and receipt of HPV vaccination, adjusting for sociodemographic and behavioral factors. Results: Among 20,917 participants (mean [SD] age (years): 31.41 [13.58]), 489 (2.0%) were cancer survivors; 99 (19.3%) received HPV vaccination. Among 20,423 adults without a cancer diagnosis, 5317 (27.8%) had received HPV vaccination. Five participants had missing data on cancer history. Cancer survivors had significantly lower odds of HPV vaccination compared to adults without a personal cancer history (Adjusted Odds Ratio [aOR]: 0.67, 95% CI: 0.52–0.87, p = 0.003). Living in non-metro areas (aOR: 0.77, 95% CI: 0.66–0.89, p

Approximation of localized surface plasmon resonance change as a biosensor in capsids of human papillomavirus types 6, 11, 16, and 18

Authors: Víctor Gómez Flores, Yazmin Molina-Arias, Gloria Erika Mejía-Carmona, Alejandra Vargas-Caraveo, Guillermo Mejía-Cisneros, Perla Guadalupe Venzor-Moncada, Sarai Esmeralda Favela-Camacho, Juan Francisco Hernandez-Paz, Víctor Gómez-Flores
Original abstract (English)
Abstract This work aims to study the localized surface plasmon resonance (LSPR) shift in gold nanoparticles and their interaction with human papillomavirus (HPV) capsids samples, with potential applications in biosensors development. Results indicate a shift in the resonance wavelength, suggesting a direct interaction that could be exploited for the design of highly sensitive sensors. This study contributes to biomedical applications for early virus detection through nanomaterials. Gold nanoparticles (AuNPs) were synthesized and their optical properties characterized using UV–Vis spectroscopy. Subsequently, the plasmon response was evaluated in the presence of HPV biomolecules. Graphical abstract Schematic overview illustrating the Turkevich synthesis of stable 13 nm gold nanoparticles (AuNPs) and their comprehensive physical–chemical characterization via UV–Vis, DRX, DLS, and SEM. The workflow highlights biological assays involving HPV capsid variants, BSA protein, and target DNA sequences, culminating in a sensitive localized surface plasmon resonance (LSPR) shift for rapid, colorimetric biosensing. A Multimethod Evaluation to Assess Feasibility, Acceptability, and Preliminary Efficacy of HPVVaxFacts, a Tailored Mobile Web App, for Parents With Unvaccinated Children: Pilot 2-Arm Randomized Controlled Trial.

摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
目的 本研究旨在评估 HPVVaxFacts 在 9-17 岁青少年家长中的可行性、可接受性和初步疗效。
设计与对象
干预后 12 个月通过电子健康记录评估 疫苗接种 率。
核心方法
使用多种方法评估评估可行性、可接受性和初步疗效(HPV 疫苗 知识、关注点、意图和 疫苗接种 率)。
主要结果

解释与创新
HPVVaxFacts (233 Analytics) 是一款新颖的移动网络应用程序,提供基于健康信念模型和合理行动理论的定制教育,解决家长在临床前就诊时关心的问题。

结论 我们的研究表明,HPVVaxFacts 提供有关 HPV 疫苗接种 的预访、定制信息是可行的,并且能够被家长接受。

A Multimethod Evaluation to Assess Feasibility, Acceptability, and Preliminary Efficacy of HPVVaxFacts, a Tailored Mobile Web App, for Parents With Unvaccinated Children: Pilot 2-Arm Randomized Controlled Trial.

Authors: Jennifer Cunningham-Erves, Pamela C. Hull, Amanda F. Dempsey, Tatsuki Koyama, Lili Sun, Mariya Chalise, Elizabeth C. Stewart, Jessica L. Jones, Douglas Landsittel, Meredith Smalls
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Mobile health (mHealth) interventions may improve provider-parent communication on human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination to reduce concerns, and increase intention and uptake. HPVVaxFacts (233 Analytics) is a novel, mobile web app delivering tailored education based on the Health Belief Model and Theory of Reasoned Action, addressing parental concerns preclinic visit. Objective This study aimed to assess the feasibility, acceptability, and preliminary efficacy of HPVVaxFacts among parents of adolescents aged 9‐17 years. Methods We conducted a pilot, randomized controlled trial in 2 urban Tennessee clinics from June to September 2023 comparing 2 groups: tailored education via HPVVaxFacts mobile web app (intervention, n=27), and nutrition education (attention control, n=30). Eligible parents had or were caregivers to a child aged 9 to 17 years unvaccinated against HPV, had a mobile phone, had an upcoming clinic visit, and spoke English. The recruitment strategy was patient intake software—Phreesia (Phreesia, Inc ) and eClinicalWorks (eClinicalWorks). Although unblinded, parents could deduce their study arm assignment. Providers were blinded. Feasibility, acceptability, and preliminary efficacy (HPV vaccine knowledge, concerns, intentions, and vaccination rates) were assessed using multimethod evaluation. Parents were assessed at baseline and immediately post intervention via surveys. Vaccination rates were assessed at 12 months post intervention via electronic health records. Nineteen parent interviews were conducted up to 9 months post intervention. A clinic staff consultation (n=6) was 1 month post intervention. Results Of 57 enrolled parents, most were female (52/57, 91%), non-Hispanic White (44/57, 77%), had ≤US $80,000 household income (32/57, 56%), and had some college or less (27/57, 47%). In total, 81% (36) of parents viewed HPVVaxFacts. Post intervention, HPV vaccine initiation was higher in the intervention group compared to the attention control group (48% vs 17%; difference 0.24; 95% CI 0.03-0.46; P =.01). Parents in the HPVVaxFacts arm demonstrated a greater reduction in knowledge (ie, knowledge increase; mean change: −0.6 vs 0.1) and concern scores (mean change: −3.4 vs −1.4) than those in the nutrition education arm. However, between-arm differences were not statistically significant ( P =.13 and P =.14, respectively). The majority found the study protocol and HPVVaxFacts acceptable. Benefits of HPVVaxFacts include confirming their decision to vaccinate, supporting parent-child discussion on the vaccine, and answering questions preclinic visit or offering questions for the provider. Study protocol delivery and mobile web app instructions were suggested areas for improvement. Barriers for HPVVaxFacts use include content in English only and digital format. Conclusions Our study suggests HPVVaxFacts was feasible and acceptable among parents to provide previsit, tailored information on HPV vaccination. Outcomes offer a positive trajectory but need more exploration. Next steps include a well-powered efficacy trial to determine the impact of HPVVaxFacts on initiation vaccine rates and parental hesitancy factors, as well as to explore an interaction, effect modification, and mediation among different variables.

Design and population
Vaccination rates were assessed at 12 months post intervention via electronic health records.
Core methods
Feasibility, acceptability, and preliminary efficacy (HPV vaccine knowledge, concerns, intentions, and vaccination rates) were assessed using multimethod evaluation.
Main results
Parents in the HPVVaxFacts arm demonstrated a greater reduction in knowledge (ie, knowledge increase; mean change: −0.6 vs 0.1) and concern scores (mean change: −3.4 vs −1.4) than those in the nutrition education arm.
